`scripts/recover_key.py`:

```py
"""Recover the 16-byte SLE key by frequency analysis, then validate it on the SLE chunks.

Cipher (from FUN_00102360): c = (p - k) ^ key[i % 16], so p = ((c ^ key[j]) + k) is wrong order;
the game *decrypts* with p = (c - k) ^ key[i % 16], k = 3 + 3*i (mod 256).
If plaintext byte p is 0 then key[i % 16] = (c - k) & 0xff, so the most frequent (c - k)
per residue class is the key byte when the plaintext has many zero bytes.
"""
import glob
import sys
from collections import Counter

sys.path.insert(0, "/work/tools")
import slz  # noqa: E402

SECTOR = 2048
ISO = glob.glob("/work/*.iso")[0]
TOC_LBA, TOC_SECTORS = 1024, 18
SLE_LBAS = (255055, 900723)


def guess_key(cipher: bytes, phase: int = 0):
    key = bytearray(16)
    conf = []
    for j in range(16):
        c = Counter()
        for i in range(j, len(cipher), 16):
            k = (3 + 3 * (i + phase)) & 0xFF
            c[(cipher[i] - k) & 0xFF] += 1
        (val, n), = c.most_common(1)
        key[(j + phase) & 0xF] = val
        conf.append(n / max(1, sum(c.values())))
    return bytes(key), conf


with open(ISO, "rb") as f:
    f.seek(TOC_LBA * SECTOR)
    toc = f.read(TOC_SECTORS * SECTOR)
    sle = {}
    for lba in SLE_LBAS:
        f.seek(lba * SECTOR)
        sle[lba] = f.read(4 * 1024 * 1024)

candidates = []
key, conf = guess_key(toc)
print("TOC-derived key:", key.hex(), "min/avg confidence: %.2f / %.2f" % (min(conf), sum(conf) / 16))
candidates.append(("toc", key))
for lba, buf in sle.items():
    comp = slz.parse_header(buf)[2]
    k2, conf2 = guess_key(buf[16:16 + comp])
    print(f"SLE@{lba}-derived key:", k2.hex(), "avg confidence %.2f" % (sum(conf2) / 16))
    candidates.append((f"sle{lba}", k2))

for name, key in candidates:
    for lba, buf in sle.items():
        try:
            parts = slz.decompress_chain(buf, 0, key)
            print(f"[{name}] SLE@{lba}: OK, {len(parts)} chunk(s), {sum(map(len, parts))} bytes, head {parts[0][:16].hex()}")
            with open(f"/work/work/sle_{lba}.bin", "wb") as o:
                o.write(b"".join(parts))
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[{name}] SLE@{lba}: fail ({str(e)[:60]})")

plain = bytes(((c - ((3 + 3 * i) & 0xFF)) & 0xFF) ^ candidates[0][1][i & 0xF] for i, c in enumerate(toc))
open("/work/work/toc_decrypted.bin", "wb").write(plain)
print("TOC decrypted head:")
for i in range(0, 128, 16):
    print(f"  {i:04x}  " + " ".join(f"{b:02x}" for b in plain[i:i + 16]))

```

`scripts/run_analyzer.sh`:

```sh
#!/bin/sh
# Build ps2_analyzer from the fork (mounted at /work) and run it on an ELF.
# Usage: run_analyzer.sh <input elf> <output toml>   (container paths)
# docker run --rm -v D:\VP2S_Recomp:/work vp2s-toolchain sh /work/scripts/run_analyzer.sh ...
set -e
FORK=/work/external/PS2Recomp
BUILD=$FORK/out/linux-analyzer
DEPS=""
for src in /work/build/deps/*-src; do
    [ -d "$src" ] || continue
    name=$(basename "$src" -src | tr 'a-z' 'A-Z')
    DEPS="$DEPS -DFETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_$name=$src"
done
mkdir -p "$BUILD"
cmake -S "$FORK" -B "$BUILD" -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release $DEPS \
    -DPS2X_BUILD_RUNTIME=OFF -DPS2X_BUILD_TEST=OFF -DPS2X_BUILD_STUDIO=OFF -DPS2X_BUILD_ANALYZER=ON \
    > "$BUILD/configure.log" 2>&1 || { tail -20 "$BUILD/configure.log"; exit 1; }
cmake --build "$BUILD" --target ps2_analyzer > "$BUILD/build.log" 2>&1 || { grep -E "error" "$BUILD/build.log" | head -20; exit 1; }
ANALYZER=$(find "$BUILD" -name ps2_analyzer -type f | head -1)
"$ANALYZER" "$1" "$2"

```

`scripts/run_recomp.sh`:

```sh
#!/bin/sh
# Run ps2_recomp on a Ghidra-exported TOML, rewriting host paths to container paths.
# Usage: run_recomp.sh <toml> <input elf> <output dir> [module name]   (container paths)
# A module name switches the recompiler to overlay module mode (module_name in [general]).
# RECOMP overrides the recompiler binary (default: /src/out/linux/ps2xRecomp/ps2_recomp).
# docker run --rm -v D:\Tools\src\PS2Recomp:/src -v D:\VP2S_Recomp:/work vp2s-toolchain sh /work/scripts/run_recomp.sh ...
set -e
TOML="$1"; INPUT="$2"; OUT="$3"; MODULE="$4"
RECOMP="${RECOMP:-/src/out/linux/ps2xRecomp/ps2_recomp}"
DIR=$(dirname "$TOML")
CSV="${TOML%.toml}_functions.csv"
FIXED="${TOML%.toml}.docker.toml"
sed -e "s|^input = .*|input = \"$INPUT\"|" \
    -e "s|^output = .*|output = \"$OUT\"|" \
    -e "s|^ghidra_output = .*|ghidra_output = \"$CSV\"|" "$TOML" > "$FIXED"
if [ -n "$MODULE" ]; then
    sed -i "/^\[general\]/a module_name = \"$MODULE\"" "$FIXED"
fi
rm -rf "$OUT"
start=$(date +%s)
cd "$DIR"
set +e
"$RECOMP" "$FIXED" > "$OUT.log" 2>&1
rc=$?
set -e
echo "exit=$rc seconds=$(( $(date +%s) - start ))"
tail -12 "$OUT.log"
echo "warnings: $(grep -ciE 'warn' "$OUT.log")  errors: $(grep -ciE 'error' "$OUT.log")"
[ -d "$OUT" ] && echo "output: $(find "$OUT" -type f | wc -l) files, $(du -sh "$OUT" | cut -f1), $(cat "$OUT"/*.cpp 2>/dev/null | wc -l) lines of C++"

```

`scripts/scan_blob.py`:

```py
"""Scan the hidden data area of the ISO (everything after the ISO9660 filesystem).

Prints the first sectors as hex, an entropy map, and offsets of known magics
(ELF/IRX modules, TIM2 textures, etc.) to help understand the archive layout.
"""
import math
import glob
import struct
import sys

SECTOR = 2048
ISO = glob.glob("/work/*.iso")[0]
FS_END = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 1681408  # from 7z "Physical Size"

MAGICS = {
    b"\x7fELF": "ELF",
    b"TIM2": "TIM2",
    b"VAGp": "VAG",
    b"IECS": "IECS(snd)",
    b"pBAV": "VAB",
    b"\x00\x00\x01\xba": "MPEG-PS",
    b"PSMF": "PSMF",
    b"RIFF": "RIFF",
}


def entropy(buf):
    if not buf:
        return 0.0
    counts = [0] * 256
    for b in buf:
        counts[b] += 1
    n = len(buf)
    return -sum(c / n * math.log2(c / n) for c in counts if c)


def hexdump(buf, base):
    for i in range(0, len(buf), 16):
        chunk = buf[i:i + 16]
        hx = " ".join(f"{b:02x}" for b in chunk)
        asc = "".join(chr(b) if 32 <= b < 127 else "." for b in chunk)
        print(f"{base + i:010x}  {hx:<47}  {asc}")


with open(ISO, "rb") as f:
    f.seek(0, 2)
    size = f.tell()
    print(f"ISO size {size} bytes, {size // SECTOR} sectors; hidden area starts at sector {FS_END // SECTOR}")

    f.seek(FS_END)
    print("=== first 512 bytes of hidden area")
    hexdump(f.read(512), FS_END)

    # Magic scan at sector starts (cheap) + entropy per 64 MiB block
    print("=== magics at sector boundaries (first 40 per type)")
    found = {}
    block = 64 * 1024 * 1024
    ent_map = []
    pos = FS_END - (FS_END % SECTOR)
    f.seek(pos)
    while True:
        data = f.read(block)
        if not data:
            break
        ent_map.append((pos, entropy(data[:1 << 20])))
        for off in range(0, len(data), SECTOR):
            head = data[off:off + 4]
            name = MAGICS.get(head)
            if name:
                lst = found.setdefault(name, [])
                if len(lst) < 40:
                    lst.append(pos + off)
                else:
                    found[name + "_more"] = found.get(name + "_more", 0) + 1
        pos += len(data)
    for k, v in found.items():
        if isinstance(v, list):
            extra = found.get(k + "_more", 0)
            print(f"{k}: {len(v) + extra} hits; first: " + ", ".join(f"0x{o:x}" for o in v[:12]))
    print("=== entropy (first MiB of each 64 MiB block, 8.0 = compressed/encrypted)")
    for p, e in ent_map:
        print(f"0x{p:09x} ({p // SECTOR:>8} lba)  {e:.2f}  " + "#" * int(e * 4))

```

`scripts/scan_chunks.py`:

```py
"""Find every SLZ/SLE chunk chain on the disc (aligned or not), classify, and extract modules.

Output:
  /work/work/chunks.tsv          one line per chain head: offset, magic, type, comp, decomp, chain len, kind, info
  /work/work/modules/<slot>_<id>_<offset>.bin   fully decompressed overlay modules
"""
import glob
import os
import struct
import sys
from collections import Counter
from multiprocessing import Pool

sys.path.insert(0, "/work/tools")
import slz  # noqa: E402

from elftools.elf.elffile import ELFFile

ISO = glob.glob("/work/*.iso")[0]
OUT_DIR = "/work/work/modules"
SIZE = os.path.getsize(ISO)
SPAN = 64 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_CHUNK = 8 * 1024 * 1024

with open("/work/extracted/SLES_546.45", "rb") as f:
    SLOTS = sorted({s["p_vaddr"] for s in ELFFile(f).iter_segments() if s["p_type"] == "PT_LOAD"} - {0x100000})


def plausible(buf, off):
    try:
        magic, typ, comp, decomp, nxt = slz.parse_header(buf, off)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if typ > 3 or not (0 < comp <= MAX_CHUNK) or not (0 < decomp <= 4 * MAX_CHUNK) or comp > decomp + 64:
        return None
    if nxt and (nxt < 16 + comp or nxt > comp + 4096):
        return None
    return magic, typ, comp, decomp, nxt


def classify(head: bytes):
    if len(head) >= 16:
        w = struct.unpack_from("<4I", head)
        if w[0] == 0 and w[2] in SLOTS:
            return "module", f"id={w[1]} slot=0x{w[2]:x} text=0x{w[3]:x}"
    if head[:4] == b"\x7fELF":
        return "elf", ""
    tag = head[:4]
    if all(32 <= b < 127 for b in tag):
        return "tagged", tag.decode()
    return "data", head[:8].hex()


def scan(start):
    results = []
    with open(ISO, "rb") as f:
        f.seek(start)
        buf = f.read(SPAN + MAX_CHUNK + 32)
    for magic in (b"SLZ", b"SLE"):
        pos = buf.find(magic)
        while 0 <= pos < SPAN:
            h = plausible(buf, pos)
            if h:
                try:
                    head = slz.decompress_chunk(buf, pos, slz.SLE_KEY, peek=64)
                    n, off = 1, pos
                    while slz.parse_header(buf, off)[4] and n < 4096:
                        off += slz.parse_header(buf, off)[4]
                        if not plausible(buf, off):
                            break
                        n += 1
                    kind, info = classify(head)
                    results.append((start + pos, h[0].decode(), h[1], h[2], h[3], n, kind, info))
                except Exception:
                    pass
            pos = buf.find(magic, pos + 1)
    return results


if __name__ == "__main__":
    os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    with Pool(os.cpu_count()) as p:
        rows = sorted(r for part in p.map(scan, range(0, SIZE, SPAN)) for r in part)

    # Drop chunks that are links inside an already-found chain
    inside = set()
    with open(ISO, "rb") as f:
        for r in rows:
            if r[5] > 1:
                f.seek(r[0])
                buf = f.read(MAX_CHUNK * 2)
                off = 0
                for _ in range(r[5] - 1):
                    off += slz.parse_header(buf, off)[4]
                    inside.add(r[0] + off)
    heads = [r for r in rows if r[0] not in inside]

    with open("/work/work/chunks.tsv", "w") as o:
        o.write("offset\tmagic\ttype\tcomp\tdecomp\tchain\tkind\tinfo\n")
        for r in heads:
            o.write("\t".join(str(x) for x in r) + "\n")

    kinds = Counter(r[6] for r in heads)
    print(f"chunk headers found: {len(rows)}, chain heads: {len(heads)}")
    print("kinds:", dict(kinds))
    print("magics:", dict(Counter(r[1] for r in heads)))
    print("total compressed: %.0f MiB" % (sum(r[3] for r in rows) / 2**20))
    tags = Counter(r[7] for r in heads if r[6] == "tagged")
    print("top tags:", tags.most_common(20))

    # Extract modules completely
    mods = [r for r in heads if r[6] == "module"]
    slot_ids = Counter()
    with open(ISO, "rb") as f:
        for r in mods:
            f.seek(r[0])
            buf = f.read(MAX_CHUNK * 4)
            data = b"".join(slz.decompress_chain(buf, 0, slz.SLE_KEY))
            w = struct.unpack_from("<4I", data)
            slot_ids[w[2]] += 1
            with open(f"{OUT_DIR}/{w[2]:08x}_{w[1]:04x}_{r[0]:010x}.bin", "wb") as o:
                o.write(data)
    print(f"modules extracted: {len(mods)}")
    for s in SLOTS:
        print(f"  slot 0x{s:08x}: {slot_ids.get(s, 0)} module(s)")

```

`scripts/scan_data_overlays.py`:

```py
"""Look for headerless data overlays of the mode-B slots (slot table entries 5..20).

A decompressed part of size <= slot capacity is attributed to slot S when many aligned
words are pointers into [S, S + len(part)). Also checks raw disc sectors for module headers
(word0 == 0, word2 == mode-B slot address).
"""
import csv
import glob
import hashlib
import os
import struct
import sys
from collections import defaultdict
from multiprocessing import Pool

sys.path.insert(0, "/work/tools")
import slz  # noqa: E402

ISO = glob.glob("/work/*.iso")[0]
OUT = "/work/work/data_overlays"
ELF = open("/work/extracted/SLES_546.45", "rb").read()
SLOT_TABLE = struct.unpack_from("<21I", ELF, 0x1C8A30 - 0x100000 + 0x2E00)
MODE_B = sorted(set(SLOT_TABLE[5:21]) - {0x35ec80})
bounds = sorted(set(a for a in SLOT_TABLE if a)) + [0x549100]
CAP = {s: bounds[bounds.index(s) + 1] - s for s in MODE_B}
CAP[0x36d800] = 0x514100 - 0x36d800


def best_slot(part):
    n = len(part) // 4
    words = struct.unpack_from(f"<{n}I", part)
    best = None
    for s in MODE_B:
        if len(part) > CAP[s]:
            continue
        inside = sum(1 for w in words if s <= w < s + len(part))
        if inside >= 8 and inside >= n * 0.02 and (best is None or inside > best[1]):
            best = (s, inside)
    return best


def work(row):
    off, chain = int(row["offset"]), int(row["chain"])
    found = []
    try:
        with open(ISO, "rb") as f:
            buf = slz.read_chain(f, off)
        pos = 0
        for n in range(chain):
            if slz.parse_header(buf, pos)[3] <= 0x1a6900:
                part = slz.decompress_chunk(buf, pos, slz.SLE_KEY)
                b = best_slot(part)
                if b:
                    found.append((off, n, b[0], b[1], part))
            nxt = slz.parse_header(buf, pos)[4]
            if not nxt:
                break
            pos += nxt
    except Exception:
        pass
    return found


def raw_headers():
    hits = []
    with open(ISO, "rb") as f:
        size = os.path.getsize(ISO)
        for start in range(0, size, 256 << 20):
            f.seek(start)
            buf = f.read((256 << 20) + 16)
            for s in MODE_B + [0x35ec80]:
                pat = struct.pack("<I", s)
                p = buf.find(pat)
                while p >= 0:
                    if p >= 8 and (p - 8) % 16 == 0 and buf[p - 8:p - 4] == bytes(4):
                        w = struct.unpack_from("<8I", buf, p - 8)
                        if 0x80 < w[3] < 0x200000 and w[4] < 0x200000:
                            hits.append((start + p - 8, w[1], hex(s), hex(w[3])))
                    p = buf.find(pat, p + 4)
    return hits


if __name__ == "__main__":
    os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
    rows = list(csv.DictReader(open("/work/work/chunks.tsv"), delimiter="\t"))
    rows.sort(key=lambda r: -int(r["comp"]))
    occ = []
    with Pool(os.cpu_count()) as p:
        for found in p.imap_unordered(work, rows, chunksize=16):
            occ += found
    uniq = {}
    by_slot = defaultdict(list)
    for off, n, s, inside, part in occ:
        h = hashlib.sha1(part).hexdigest()[:10]
        if h not in uniq:
            uniq[h] = (s, len(part), inside, off, n)
            by_slot[s].append((len(part), inside))
            open(f"{OUT}/{s:08x}_{h}.bin", "wb").write(part)
    print(f"data overlay candidates: {len(occ)} occurrences, {len(uniq)} unique")
    for s in MODE_B:
        items = by_slot.get(s, [])
        print(f"  slot 0x{s:08x} cap {CAP[s]:#8x}: {len(items):>3} unique"
              + (f", sizes {min(i[0] for i in items):#x}..{max(i[0] for i in items):#x}" if items else ""))
    for h, (s, size, inside, off, n) in list(uniq.items())[:8]:
        print(f"   e.g. slot 0x{s:x} size {size:#x} pointers {inside} from chain {off} part {n}")
    rh = raw_headers()
    print(f"raw module headers on disc for mode-B slots: {len(rh)}", rh[:10])

```

`scripts/scan_grp.py`:

```py
"""Find tagged containers (raw on disc) and extract their GRP entries (small overlays).

Container (from FUN_00422c80): +0x4 entry count; entries of 16 bytes from +0x10:
tag (u32, ASCII), param, size, offset from container start. GRP: overlay loaded by
FUN_00410cc0 into slot table entry (param + 3); its payload is an SLZ chunk.
"""
import glob
import hashlib
import os
import struct
import sys
from collections import Counter, defaultdict

sys.path.insert(0, "/work/tools")
import slz  # noqa: E402

ISO = glob.glob("/work/*.iso")[0]
OUT = "/work/work/grp"
ELF = open("/work/extracted/SLES_546.45", "rb").read()
SLOT_TABLE = struct.unpack_from("<21I", ELF, 0x1C8A30 - 0x100000 + 0x2E00)
SPAN = 256 << 20
TAIL = 16 << 20


def tag_ok(t: bytes):
    return all(48 <= b < 91 or b == 0 for b in t) and t[0] != 0


def find_base(buf, pos):
    """Walk back from an entry tag to the container header."""
    for j in range(0, 512):
        base = pos - 0x10 - 16 * j
        if base < 0:
            return None
        if not tag_ok(buf[base + 0x10 + 16 * j:base + 0x14 + 16 * j]):
            return None
        count = struct.unpack_from("<I", buf, base + 4)[0]
        if struct.unpack_from("<I", buf, base)[0] == 0 and j < count <= 4096:
            if all(tag_ok(buf[base + 0x10 + 16 * k:base + 0x14 + 16 * k]) for k in range(min(count, 64))):
                return base, count
    return None


containers = {}
with open(ISO, "rb") as f:
    size = os.path.getsize(ISO)
    for start in range(0, size, SPAN):
        f.seek(start)
        buf = f.read(SPAN + TAIL)
        pos = buf.find(b"GRP\0")
        while 0 <= pos < SPAN:
            r = find_base(buf, pos)
            if r and start + r[0] not in containers:
                base, count = r
                entries = [struct.unpack_from("<4sIII", buf, base + 0x10 + 16 * k) for k in range(count)]
                containers[start + base] = (count, entries, buf[base:base + max(e[3] + e[2] for e in entries)])
            pos = buf.find(b"GRP\0", pos + 4)

os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
tags = Counter()
uniq = {}
by_slot = defaultdict(list)
bad = 0
for cbase, (count, entries, blob) in sorted(containers.items()):
    for tag, param, esize, eoff in entries:
        tags[tag.rstrip(b"\0").decode()] += 1
        if tag != b"GRP\0":
            continue
        try:
            data = slz.decompress_chunk(blob, eoff, slz.SLE_KEY)
        except Exception:
            data = blob[eoff:eoff + esize]
            if slz.HEADER.size and blob[eoff:eoff + 3] in (b"SLZ", b"SLE"):
                bad += 1
                continue
        slot = SLOT_TABLE[param + 3] if param + 3 < len(SLOT_TABLE) else None
        h = hashlib.sha1(data).hexdigest()[:10]
        if h not in uniq:
            uniq[h] = (param, slot, len(data))
            by_slot[param].append(len(data))
            open(f"{OUT}/{param:02d}_{(slot or 0):08x}_{h}.bin", "wb").write(data)

print(f"containers with GRP: {len(containers)}")
print("entry tags:", tags.most_common(25))
print(f"GRP payloads: {sum(1 for c in containers.values() for e in c[1] if e[0] == b'GRP' + bytes(1))}, unique {len(uniq)}, decode errors {bad}")
for param in sorted(by_slot):
    slot = SLOT_TABLE[param + 3] if param + 3 < len(SLOT_TABLE) else 0
    sizes = by_slot[param]
    print(f"  param {param:2} -> slot [{param + 3}] 0x{slot:08x}: {len(sizes):>3} unique, {min(sizes):#x}..{max(sizes):#x}, {sum(sizes) / 1024:.0f} KiB")

```

`scripts/scan_inner.py`:

```py
"""Decompress every chunk chain and extract MWo3 overlays found anywhere inside (and in raw sectors).

MWo3 header (0x80 bytes): 'MWo3', id, load address, text size, data size, bss size,
entry, entry2, name[0x20..]. Unique overlays are written to /work/work/overlays/.
"""
import csv
import glob
import hashlib
import os
import struct
import sys
from multiprocessing import Pool

sys.path.insert(0, "/work/tools")
import slz  # noqa: E402

ISO = glob.glob("/work/*.iso")[0]
OUT = "/work/work/overlays"
MW = struct.Struct("<4s7I")


def overlays_in(data: bytes, where: str):
    found = []
    pos = data.find(b"MWo3")
    while pos >= 0:
        if pos + 0x80 <= len(data):
            _, ov_id, load, text, dsize, bss, entry, _ = MW.unpack_from(data, pos)
            if 0x100000 <= load < 0x2000000 and text < 0x400000 and dsize < 0x400000:
                name = data[pos + 0x20:pos + 0x60].split(b"\0")[0].decode("ascii", "replace")
                blob = data[pos:pos + 0x80 + text + dsize]
                found.append((where, ov_id, load, text, dsize, bss, name, blob))
        pos = data.find(b"MWo3", pos + 4)
    return found


def work(row):
    off, comp, chain = int(row["offset"]), int(row["comp"]), int(row["chain"])
    try:
        with open(ISO, "rb") as f:
            f.seek(off)
            buf = f.read(comp * chain + 4096 * chain + 65536)
        data = b"".join(slz.decompress_chain(buf, 0, slz.SLE_KEY))
    except Exception:
        return [], 0, 1
    return overlays_in(data, f"chunk@{off}"), len(data), 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
    rows = list(csv.DictReader(open("/work/work/chunks.tsv"), delimiter="\t"))
    rows.sort(key=lambda r: -int(r["comp"]))  # big ones first for load balance
    total_out = errors = 0
    hits = []
    with Pool(os.cpu_count()) as p:
        for found, n, err in p.imap_unordered(work, rows, chunksize=16):
            hits += found
            total_out += n
            errors += err

    # Raw (uncompressed) occurrences straight on the disc
    size = os.path.getsize(ISO)
    with open(ISO, "rb") as f:
        for start in range(0, size, 256 << 20):
            f.seek(start)
            hits += overlays_in(f.read((256 << 20) + 0x400000), f"raw@{start}")

    uniq = {}
    for where, ov_id, load, text, dsize, bss, name, blob in hits:
        h = hashlib.sha1(blob).hexdigest()[:10]
        if h not in uniq:
            uniq[h] = (load, ov_id, name, text, dsize, bss, where)
            safe = "".join(c if c.isalnum() or c in "._-" else "_" for c in name) or "noname"
            open(f"{OUT}/{load:08x}_{ov_id:04x}_{safe}_{h}.bin", "wb").write(blob)

    print(f"decompressed {total_out / 2**30:.2f} GiB from {len(rows)} chains ({errors} errors)")
    print(f"MWo3 occurrences: {len(hits)}, unique overlays: {len(uniq)}")
    per_slot = {}
    for load, ov_id, name, text, dsize, bss, where in uniq.values():
        per_slot.setdefault(load, []).append((ov_id, name, text))
    for load in sorted(per_slot):
        items = sorted(per_slot[load])
        names = ", ".join(n for _, n, _ in items[:6])
        print(f"  slot 0x{load:08x}: {len(items):>3} unique, text {sum(t for *_, t in items) / 1024:.0f} KiB  e.g. {names}")

```

`scripts/scan_nested.py`:

```py
"""Look for small-slot modules nested inside decompressed parts.

For each decompressed part: (1) raw module headers (word0 == 0, word2 == small slot address,
word6/7 constructor table inside that slot) and (2) nested SLZ/SLE chunks whose decompressed
data starts with such a header. Unique hits go to /work/work/nested/.
"""
import csv
import glob
import hashlib
import os
import struct
import sys
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from multiprocessing import Pool

sys.path.insert(0, "/work/tools")
import slz  # noqa: E402

ISO = glob.glob("/work/*.iso")[0]
OUT = "/work/work/nested"
ELF = open("/work/extracted/SLES_546.45", "rb").read()
SLOT_TABLE = struct.unpack_from("<21I", ELF, 0x1C8A30 - 0x100000 + 0x2E00)
SMALL = sorted(set(SLOT_TABLE[7:21]))
LO, HI = min(SMALL), 0x549100


def module_at(data, p):
    if p < 0 or p + 0x80 > len(data):
        return None
    w = struct.unpack_from("<8I", data, p)
    if w[0] == 0 and w[2] in SMALL and 0x80 < w[3] < 0x200000 and w[4] < 0x200000 \
            and LO <= w[6] <= w[7] < HI:
        return w
    return None


def scan_part(data, where, found):
    for slot in SMALL:
        pat = struct.pack("<I", slot)
        p = data.find(pat)
        while p >= 0:
            w = module_at(data, p - 8)
            if w:
                found.append((where + f"+raw@{p - 8:#x}", w, data[p - 8:p - 8 + w[3] + w[4]]))
            p = data.find(pat, p + 4)
    for magic in (b"SLZ", b"SLE"):
        p = data.find(magic)
        while p >= 0:
            try:
                magic_, typ, comp, decomp, nxt = slz.parse_header(data, p)
                if typ <= 3 and 0 < comp <= len(data) - p and 0x80 <= decomp < 0x400000:
                    head = slz.decompress_chunk(data, p, slz.SLE_KEY, peek=0x80)
                    if module_at(head, 0):
                        full = slz.decompress_chunk(data, p, slz.SLE_KEY)
                        found.append((where + f"+slz@{p:#x}", module_at(full, 0), full))
            except Exception:
                pass
            p = data.find(magic, p + 1)


def work(row):
    off, chain = int(row["offset"]), int(row["chain"])
    found, tags = [], Counter()
    try:
        with open(ISO, "rb") as f:
            buf = slz.read_chain(f, off)
        pos = 0
        for n in range(chain):
            part = slz.decompress_chunk(buf, pos, slz.SLE_KEY)
            before = len(found)
            scan_part(part, f"{off}/{n}", found)
            if len(found) > before:
                tags[part[:4]] += 1
            nxt = slz.parse_header(buf, pos)[4]
            if not nxt:
                break
            pos += nxt
    except Exception:
        pass
    return found, tags


if __name__ == "__main__":
    os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
    rows = list(csv.DictReader(open("/work/work/chunks.tsv"), delimiter="\t"))
    rows.sort(key=lambda r: -int(r["comp"]))
    occ, tags = [], Counter()
    with Pool(os.cpu_count()) as p:
        for found, t in p.imap_unordered(work, rows, chunksize=16):
            occ += found
            tags += t

    uniq = {}
    with open("/work/work/nested.tsv", "w") as o:
        o.write("where\tid\tslot\ttext\tdata\tbss\tsha1\n")
        for where, w, blob in occ:
            h = hashlib.sha1(blob).hexdigest()[:10]
            o.write(f"{where}\t{w[1]}\t0x{w[2]:x}\t{w[3]:#x}\t{w[4]:#x}\t{w[5]:#x}\t{h}\n")
            if h not in uniq:
                uniq[h] = w
                open(f"{OUT}/{w[2]:08x}_{w[1]:04x}_{h}.bin", "wb").write(blob)

    by_slot = defaultdict(list)
    for w in uniq.values():
        by_slot[w[2]].append(w)
    print(f"nested module hits: {len(occ)}, unique {len(uniq)}")
    print("container tags holding them:", [(t.hex(), t, n) for t, n in tags.most_common(10)])
    for idx in range(7, 21):
        s = SLOT_TABLE[idx]
        ws = by_slot.get(s, [])
        ids = sorted({w[1] for w in ws})
        print(f"  [{idx:2}] slot 0x{s:08x}: {len(ws):>3} unique"
              + (f", ids {ids[0]}..{ids[-1]}, text {sum(w[3] for w in ws) / 1024:.0f} KiB" if ws else ""))

```

`scripts/scan_parts.py`:

```py
"""Decompress each chunk of every chain separately and extract module-headed parts.

A part is a module when its header (0x80 bytes, kept in memory at the slot address) has
word0 in {0, 'MWo3'} and word2 equal to a slot address from the slot table at 0x1c8a30.
Large parts that do not match are listed too, to spot headerless modules.
Unique modules go to /work/work/parts/<slot>_<id>_<sha1>.bin; a TSV lists every occurrence.
"""
import csv
import glob
import hashlib
import os
import struct
import sys
from collections import defaultdict
from multiprocessing import Pool

sys.path.insert(0, "/work/tools")
import slz  # noqa: E402

ISO = glob.glob("/work/*.iso")[0]
OUT = "/work/work/parts"
ELF = open("/work/extracted/SLES_546.45", "rb").read()
SLOT_TABLE = struct.unpack_from("<21I", ELF, 0x1C8A30 - 0x100000 + 0x2E00)
SLOTS = {a for a in SLOT_TABLE if a and a != 0x100000}


def work(row):
    off, chain = int(row["offset"]), int(row["chain"])
    found = []
    try:
        with open(ISO, "rb") as f:
            buf = slz.read_chain(f, off)
        pos = 0
        for n in range(chain):
            part = slz.decompress_chunk(buf, pos, slz.SLE_KEY)
            if len(part) >= 0x80:
                w = struct.unpack_from("<8I", part)
                if w[0] in (0, 0x336F574D) and w[2] in SLOTS:
                    found.append((off, n, w[1], w[2], len(part), part))
                elif len(part) >= 0x40000:
                    print(f"large non-module part: chain {off} part {n} size {len(part):#x} head {part[:16].hex()}", flush=True)
            nxt = slz.parse_header(buf, pos)[4]
            if not nxt:
                break
            pos += nxt
    except Exception:
        pass
    return found


if __name__ == "__main__":
    os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
    rows = list(csv.DictReader(open("/work/work/chunks.tsv"), delimiter="\t"))
    rows.sort(key=lambda r: -int(r["comp"]))
    occ = []
    with Pool(os.cpu_count()) as p:
        for found in p.imap_unordered(work, rows, chunksize=16):
            occ += found

    uniq = {}
    with open("/work/work/parts.tsv", "w") as o:
        o.write("chain_offset\tpart\tid\tslot\tsize\tsha1\n")
        for off, n, mid, slot, size, part in sorted(occ, key=lambda x: (x[0], x[1])):
            h = hashlib.sha1(part).hexdigest()[:10]
            o.write(f"{off}\t{n}\t{mid}\t0x{slot:x}\t{size}\t{h}\n")
            if h not in uniq:
                uniq[h] = (slot, mid, size)
                open(f"{OUT}/{slot:08x}_{mid:04x}_{h}.bin", "wb").write(part)

    by_slot = defaultdict(list)
    for slot, mid, size in uniq.values():
        by_slot[slot].append((mid, size))
    print(f"module parts: {len(occ)} occurrences, {len(uniq)} unique")
    for idx, slot in enumerate(SLOT_TABLE):
        if slot in by_slot:
            items = by_slot[slot]
            ids = sorted({m for m, _ in items})
            print(f"  [{idx:2}] slot 0x{slot:08x}: {len(items):>3} unique, ids {ids[:12]}{'...' if len(ids) > 12 else ''}, max {max(s for _, s in items):#x}")

```

`scripts/scan_raw_overlays.py`:

```py
"""Find headerless code overlays for the small slots (slot table entries 8..20).

The module-3 loader (FUN_00410c40) decompresses a chunk straight to the slot address with no
header handling, so these overlays are raw code. A decompressed part of size <= slot capacity
is attributed to slot S when its jal targets land on function prologues, either inside the
part placed at S or inside the main ELF.
"""
import csv
import glob
import hashlib
import os
import struct
import sys
from collections import defaultdict
from multiprocessing import Pool

sys.path.insert(0, "/work/tools")
import slz  # noqa: E402

ISO = glob.glob("/work/*.iso")[0]
OUT = "/work/work/raw_overlays"
ELF = open("/work/extracted/SLES_546.45", "rb").read()
MAIN = ELF[0x2E00:0x2E00 + 0xc8b00]
MAIN_W = struct.unpack_from(f"<{len(MAIN) // 4}I", MAIN)
SLOT_TABLE = struct.unpack_from("<21I", ELF, 0x1C8A30 - 0x100000 + 0x2E00)
SMALL = sorted(set(SLOT_TABLE[8:21]))
CAP = {s: (sorted(SMALL + [0x549100])[sorted(SMALL + [0x549100]).index(s) + 1] - s) for s in SMALL}


def is_prologue(w):
    return (w >> 16) == 0x27BD and (w & 0x8000)


def score(part: bytes, slot: int):
    words = struct.unpack_from(f"<{len(part) // 4}I", part)
    inner = inner_ok = main_ok = jals = 0
    for w in words:
        if w >> 26 != 3:
            continue
        jals += 1
        t = (w & 0x3FFFFFF) << 2
        if slot <= t < slot + len(part):
            inner += 1
            inner_ok += bool(is_prologue(words[(t - slot) // 4]))
        elif 0x100000 <= t < 0x100000 + len(MAIN):
            main_ok += bool(is_prologue(MAIN_W[(t - 0x100000) // 4]))
    return jals, inner, inner_ok, main_ok


def work(row):
    off, comp, chain = int(row["offset"]), int(row["comp"]), int(row["chain"])
    found = []
    try:
        with open(ISO, "rb") as f:
            f.seek(off)
            buf = f.read(comp * chain + 4096 * chain + 65536)
        pos = 0
        for n in range(chain):
            decomp = slz.parse_header(buf, pos)[3]
            if 0x100 <= decomp <= 0x10000:
                part = slz.decompress_chunk(buf, pos, slz.SLE_KEY)
                best = None
                for s in SMALL:
                    if len(part) > CAP[s]:
                        continue
                    jals, inner, inner_ok, main_ok = score(part, s)
                    good = inner_ok + main_ok
                    if jals >= 4 and good >= 0.6 * jals and inner_ok >= 1:
                        if best is None or inner_ok > best[1]:
                            best = (s, inner_ok, main_ok, jals)
                if best:
                    found.append((off, n, best, part))
            nxt = slz.parse_header(buf, pos)[4]
            if not nxt:
                break
            pos += nxt
    except Exception:
        pass
    return found


if __name__ == "__main__":
    os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
    rows = list(csv.DictReader(open("/work/work/chunks.tsv"), delimiter="\t"))
    rows.sort(key=lambda r: -int(r["comp"]))
    occ = []
    with Pool(os.cpu_count()) as p:
        for found in p.imap_unordered(work, rows, chunksize=16):
            occ += found

    uniq = {}
    with open("/work/work/raw_overlays.tsv", "w") as o:
        o.write("chain_offset\tpart\tslot\tsize\tinner_ok\tmain_ok\tjals\tsha1\n")
        for off, n, (s, inner_ok, main_ok, jals), part in sorted(occ, key=lambda x: (x[0], x[1])):
            h = hashlib.sha1(part).hexdigest()[:10]
            o.write(f"{off}\t{n}\t0x{s:x}\t{len(part)}\t{inner_ok}\t{main_ok}\t{jals}\t{h}\n")
            if h not in uniq:
                uniq[h] = (s, len(part))
                open(f"{OUT}/{s:08x}_{h}.bin", "wb").write(part)

    by_slot = defaultdict(list)
    for s, size in uniq.values():
        by_slot[s].append(size)
    print(f"raw overlay candidates: {len(occ)} occurrences, {len(uniq)} unique")
    for idx in range(8, 21):
        s = SLOT_TABLE[idx]
        sizes = by_slot.get(s, [])
        print(f"  [{idx:2}] slot 0x{s:08x} cap {CAP[s]:#7x}: {len(sizes):>3} unique"
              + (f", sizes {min(sizes):#x}..{max(sizes):#x}, code {sum(sizes) / 1024:.0f} KiB" if sizes else ""))

```

`scripts/sle_failures.py`:

```py
"""List every plausible SLE header on the disc and whether our key decrypts it."""
import glob
import os
import struct
import sys
from multiprocessing import Pool

sys.path.insert(0, "/work/tools")
import slz  # noqa: E402

ISO = glob.glob("/work/*.iso")[0]
SIZE = os.path.getsize(ISO)
SPAN = 64 << 20


def scan(start):
    out = []
    with open(ISO, "rb") as f:
        f.seek(start)
        buf = f.read(SPAN + 16)
    p = buf.find(b"SLE")
    while 0 <= p < SPAN:
        magic, typ, comp, decomp, nxt = struct.unpack_from("<3sBIII", buf + bytes(16), p)
        if typ <= 3 and 0 < comp <= 16 << 20 and 0 < decomp <= 64 << 20:
            with open(ISO, "rb") as f:
                f.seek(start + p)
                chunk = f.read(16 + comp)
            try:
                slz.decompress_chunk(chunk, 0, slz.SLE_KEY)
                ok = True
            except Exception:
                ok = False
            out.append((start + p, typ, comp, decomp, nxt, ok))
        p = buf.find(b"SLE", p + 1)
    return out


if __name__ == "__main__":
    with Pool(os.cpu_count()) as pool:
        rows = sorted(r for part in pool.map(scan, range(0, SIZE, SPAN)) for r in part)
    good = [r for r in rows if r[5]]
    bad = [r for r in rows if not r[5]]
    print(f"plausible SLE headers: {len(rows)}, decrypt OK: {len(good)}, FAIL: {len(bad)}")
    for r in bad[:30]:
        print(f"  offset {r[0]} (lba {r[0] // 2048}, +{r[0] % 2048:#x}) type {r[1]} comp {r[2]:#x} decomp {r[3]:#x} next {r[4]:#x}")

```

`scripts/test_slz.py`:

```py
"""Decompress every sector-aligned SLZ/SLE chain on the disc and report results.

Writes decompressed chains to /work/work/slz_samples/<lba>_<n>.bin for inspection.
"""
import glob
import os
import sys
from collections import Counter

sys.path.insert(0, "/work/tools")
import slz  # noqa: E402

SECTOR = 2048
ISO = glob.glob("/work/*.iso")[0]
OUT = "/work/work/slz_samples"
os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)

ok = Counter()
fail = []
magic_seen = Counter()
with open(ISO, "rb") as f:
    lba = 0
    while True:
        block = f.read(SECTOR * 8192)
        if not block:
            break
        for off in range(0, len(block), SECTOR):
            if block[off:off + 3] not in (b"SLZ", b"SLE"):
                continue
            here = lba + off // SECTOR
            magic_seen[block[off:off + 3]] += 1
            f_pos = f.tell()
            f.seek(here * SECTOR)
            buf = f.read(4 * 1024 * 1024)
            f.seek(f_pos)
            try:
                parts = slz.decompress_chain(buf)
                for n, p in enumerate(parts):
                    with open(f"{OUT}/{here}_{n}.bin", "wb") as o:
                        o.write(p)
                typ = buf[3]
                ok[typ] += 1
            except Exception as e:  # report and continue
                fail.append((here, buf[:4], str(e)[:80]))
        lba += len(block) // SECTOR

print("magics:", dict(magic_seen))
print("decompressed OK by first-chunk type:", dict(ok))
print(f"failures: {len(fail)}")
for x in fail[:15]:
    print("  ", x)

```

`toolchain/Dockerfile`:

```

        7zip \

`tools/README.md`:

```md
# Tools

Workshop tools, run once on the development machine. They are not part of the port: the port will be native code produced by static recompilation of the game's MIPS code. `extract.py` supplies the recompiler with the modules' code, which is encrypted and compressed on the disc.

## `pipeline.py` — from the ISO to recompiled C++

Orchestrator, to be run on Windows from the project root (Python 3.10 or later, no dependencies). Everything runs natively, without Docker:

```bash
python tools/pipeline.py
```

| Stage | Role | Output (`build/`) |
|---|---|---|
| `extract` | `extract.py` | `extract/` |
| `wrap` | copies the main ELF, runs `wrap_elf.py` on each module; the static initializers (end of `.data`, before the header's constructor table) become an executable `.init` section | `elf/main.elf`, `elf/modNNN.elf` |
| `ghidra` | headless Ghidra analysis (`r5900` processor forced) and PS2Recomp export, in a single session | `ghidra/export/<name>.toml`, `<name>_functions.csv` |
| `tool` | builds `ps2_recomp` and `ps2_analyzer` from the fork (`external/PS2Recomp`) with MSVC | `tests/` |
| `sdk` | `ps2_analyzer` identifies Sony SDK functions by signature, to bind them to the runtime's C++ implementations | `sdk/<name>.toml` |
| `entries` | `find_entry_points.py`: functions Ghidra does not see (reached through a pointer, including from another file, such as a vtable in the main ELF pointing into the core module; module constructors; main ELF functions called only from an overlay); they become recompiler `entry_points` | `entries/<name>.txt` |
| `recomp` | `ps2_recomp` on each input, with the Ghidra map and the SDK bindings merged (at the same address, the SDK name wins); modules are built in module mode (`module_name = "modNNN"`). Output goes through `recomp-staging/` and only modified files are copied over, so that MSVC only rebuilds what changed | `recomp/<name>/`, `recomp/<name>.log` |
| `vu` | `ps2_vu_aot` on each input: its VU1 microcode compiled ahead of time. By default in fast mode (`PS2X_VU_AOT_FAST`: each block's static schedule, relaxed floats; VU1 about 25% faster, the same GS packets on recorded field runs); `VP2S_VU_EXACT=1` keeps the exact pipeline model. Changing the mode regenerates the fragments | `vu_aot/<name>/` |
| `report` | per-input figures and totals | `report.md`, `report.json` |
| `port` | builds `port/` (fork runtime + generated code) with MSVC (VS 2022 Build Tools, located via `vswhere` or `VP2S_VS_DIR`) | `port/vp2s.exe` |
| `test` | builds and runs the fork's test suites with MSVC (`ps2x_tests` and the 4 IOP suites) | `tests/<suite>.log` |

Running the game: `build\port\vp2s.exe build\extract\iso\SLES_546.45`. The ISO image is looked up via `VP2S_ISO`, otherwise by walking up from the ELF's folder.

Diagnostics and runtime options:

- `VP2S_DIAG=1` prints every 5 seconds the PC, the resident modules per slot, the game threads (state, wait, PC), the state of the sound worker and, with the `[vp2s-iop]` prefix, the IOP side: modules loaded via SIF, RPC clients and servers, state of the IOP emulator and its threads, latest RPC calls; with the `[vp2s-gs]` prefix, the GS side: display registers, presented buffer, non-black pixels per buffer, recent GS activity. It also saves a screenshot of the game screen (`vp2s_diag_N.png`), taken directly from the image produced by the runtime's GS, even when the window is minimized.
- **F9** saves a screenshot at any time (`vp2s_shot_N.png`).
- `VP2S_DUMP=<address>:<size>` writes that memory range to `vp2s_dump.bin`, and `VP2S_WATCH=<address>,<address>…` prints those memory words on each pass.
- Video decoding goes through FFmpeg (`-DVP2S_FFMPEG=OFF` to remove it): on the first build, the fork downloads FFmpeg 7.1 LGPL as DLLs (35 MB) into `build/port/ThirdParty`.
- `VP2S_PROFILE=<seconds>` samples the game threads about 1,000 times per second and prints at that interval (`[vp2s-prof]` prefix) the functions where the busiest thread spends its time, exclusive and inclusive. Names come from the public symbols of `vp2s.pdb`, produced at link time without recompiling. Static functions are attributed to the preceding public symbol, hence a few misleading names (`std::invalid_argument`…). Sampling slows the game down: do not measure speed at the same time. Other threads busy more than 30% of the time (the GS drawing threads) are also listed, with their main functions.
- The port enables the fork's asynchronous GS drawing (`GSCpuBackend::SetAsyncRasterization`): primitives are drawn by dedicated threads, which makes direct VRAM reads (the non-black pixel diagnostic) approximate while a draw is in progress. `VP2S_GS_SYNC=1` reverts to immediate drawing on the game thread, for comparison.
- The runtime debugger (ImGui) opens with **F1**; it is built by default (`-DVP2S_DEBUG_UI=OFF` to remove it).

SDK functions that the signature database does not recognize are bound manually in `MANUAL_BINDINGS` (`tools/pipeline.py`), for example the `sceSifLoadModuleBuffer` wrapper at `0x1400f8`. The `recomp` stage regenerates an input as soon as its configuration changes.

Each stage skips work whose outputs are newer than its inputs. Options: `--only <stage>`, `--from <stage>`, `--force`, `--inputs main,mod003`.

Prerequisites: Visual Studio 2022 Build Tools, Ghidra (`GHIDRA_INSTALL_DIR`), and the Emotion Engine extension **zip** for that Ghidra version (`VP2S_GHIDRA_EE_ZIP`, by default in `D:\Tools\downloads`). The optional `build/deps` folder holds the sources of the CMake dependencies (`<name>-src`), for building without network access.

> Why the extension zip: when launched through the Windows `python` alias (Store or Python install manager), Python and its child processes see a redirected `%APPDATA%`. Ghidra does not find the installed extensions there and **silently** falls back to a generic MIPS64 language. The orchestrator therefore gives Ghidra its own settings folder (`XDG_CONFIG_HOME` → `build/ghidra/settings`), installs the extension there, and refuses to continue if any language other than `r5900` appears in the log.

To build another state of the fork (a git worktree, for example) without touching the default folders, `pipeline.py` reads `VP2S_FORK` (fork folder), `VP2S_TEST_BUILD` and `VP2S_PORT_BUILD` (names of the build folders under `build/`):

```bash
VP2S_FORK=D:/VP2S_Recomp/build/wt/gs-fast VP2S_PORT_BUILD=port-gs python tools/pipeline.py --only port
```

An FFmpeg archive already downloaded by another build of the port is reused. The `test` stage only builds the suites defined by the selected fork.

## GS recordings and `ps2_gs_replay`

`PS2X_GS_RECORD=<file>` (with `PS2X_GS_RECORD_AT=<seconds>`, default 0, and `PS2X_GS_RECORD_SECONDS=<seconds>`, default 10) records the GS drawing state and VRAM, then every GIF packet and image upload. The fork's `ps2_gs_replay` tool (built in `build/tests/ps2xRuntime/`) replays a recording on a fresh GS in a fraction of a second:

```bash
ps2_gs_replay field.gsr --list
ps2_gs_replay field.gsr --until 793 --dump scene=200,8,0,512,480
```

`--list` prints every command, `--until N` replays commands 0 to N-1 only, `--dump NAME=FBP,FBW,PSM,WIDTH,HEIGHT` writes a buffer as `NAME.ppm`, and `PS2X_GS_ASYNC_RASTER=1` rasterizes on worker threads like the game. Recordings contain game data (textures, VRAM): keep them under `build/`, never commit them.

## `extract.py` — disc extractor

Python 3.10 or later, no dependencies (no 7-Zip, no third-party packages).

```bash
python -I tools/extract.py "Valkyrie Profile 2 - Silmeria (France).iso" out
```

Add `--assets` to also decompress all the data (≈ 2.7 GiB, about 1 minute). `--jobs N` limits the number of processes.

Contents of `out/`:

| Path | Contents |
|---|---|
| `iso/` | ISO9660 files: `SLES_546.45`, `IOPRP300.IMG`, `SYSTEM.CNF` |
| `modules/` | the 25 code modules, decrypted and decompressed, **as laid out in memory**: the 0x80-byte header is at the load address, the code at `+0x80` |
| `modules.json` | for each module: identifier, original name, address, slots, sizes (code, data, BSS), constructors, hash, occurrences on the disc, and consistency with the segment declared in the ELF |
| `iop/` | the 10 IOP modules (`sio2man`, `mcman`, `Csm_`/`Csd_`/`Csi_`…) |
| `chunks.tsv` | index of the 25,558 SLZ/SLE chains: offset, LBA, number of chunks, type of each chunk |
| `assets/` | (`--assets`) each decompressed chunk, `<offset>_<chunk>.<type>` |
| `manifest.json` | summary, errors, and slots found / declared |

Expected result on SLES-54645: 25,642 headers, 25,558 chains, 0 errors, 25 modules (all matching their ELF segment), 10 IOP modules.

## `wrap_elf.py` — module → ELF

Wraps an extracted module in a minimal R5900 ELF, loaded at its address, with the module header in memory (`.modhdr` section, which acts as the guard for PS2Recomp's module mode), followed by `.text`, `.data` and `.bss` as described by the header.

## `slz.py` — SLZ compression / SLE encryption

Library used by the extractor: SLZ types 0 to 3, SLE decryption (the key is not provided: `VP2S_SLE_KEY` variable or `build/sle_key.txt` file, 32 hexadecimal digits; see `load_sle_key`), reading of block chains. The formats are described in `docs/AUDIT.md`, sections 7.1 to 7.3.

> `out/`, `work/` and `extracted/` contain game data: never version or redistribute them.

```

`tools/extract.py`:

```py
"""Valkyrie Profile 2: Silmeria (SLES-54645) disc extractor.

Reads the ISO directly (no 7-Zip, no third-party Python packages) and writes:

  <out>/iso/            files of the ISO9660 filesystem (SLES_546.45, IOPRP300.IMG, SYSTEM.CNF)
  <out>/modules/        every unique code module / overlay, decrypted and decompressed,
                        exactly as the game places it in memory (0x80-byte header first)
  <out>/modules.json    module metadata: slot, id, name, sizes, constructors, occurrences
  <out>/iop/            IOP modules (IRX) shipped in the archive
  <out>/chunks.tsv      index of every SLZ/SLE chain on the disc and what each part holds
  <out>/assets/         (with --assets) every decompressed part, ~2.7 GiB
  <out>/manifest.json   summary and validation results

Usage:
  python3 -I tools/extract.py <game.iso> <out dir> [--assets] [--jobs N]

Formats are documented in docs/AUDIT.md (sections 7.1 to 7.3).
"""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
import struct
import sys
import time
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from multiprocessing import Pool

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import slz  # noqa: E402

VERSION = 1
SECTOR = 2048
SPAN = 64 << 20               # scan window per worker task
MAX_COMP = 16 << 20           # sanity bounds for chunk headers
MAX_DECOMP = 64 << 20
MAX_LINK_GAP = 4096           # 'next' may skip at most this much padding after a payload
MWO3 = 0x336F574D             # 'MWo3' (Metrowerks overlay header)
SLOT_TABLE_VADDR = 0x1C8A30   # 21 slot addresses in the main ELF (European; game_layout finds it)
SLOT_COUNT = 21
SHT_SCE_IOPMOD = 0x70000080
ET_SCE_IOPRELEXEC = 0xFF80


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- ISO9660

def iso_root_files(f):
    """List (name, lba, size) of the files in the ISO9660 root directory."""
    f.seek(16 * SECTOR)
    pvd = f.read(SECTOR)
    if pvd[1:6] != b"CD001":
        raise SystemExit("not an ISO9660 image")
    root_lba, root_size = struct.unpack_from("<I", pvd, 156 + 2)[0], struct.unpack_from("<I", pvd, 156 + 10)[0]
    f.seek(root_lba * SECTOR)
    data = f.read(root_size)
    files = []
    pos = 0
    while pos < len(data):
        length = data[pos]
        if length == 0:  # records never cross sectors: skip padding
            pos = (pos // SECTOR + 1) * SECTOR
            continue
        lba, size = struct.unpack_from("<I", data, pos + 2)[0], struct.unpack_from("<I", data, pos + 10)[0]
        flags, name_len = data[pos + 25], data[pos + 32]
        name = data[pos + 33:pos + 33 + name_len]
        if name not in (b"\0", b"\1") and not flags & 2:
            files.append((name.decode("ascii").split(";")[0], lba, size))
        pos += length
    return files


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- ELF

def elf_segments(elf: bytes):
    """PT_LOAD program headers as (offset, vaddr, filesz, memsz)."""
    phoff = struct.unpack_from("<I", elf, 0x1C)[0]
    phentsize, phnum = struct.unpack_from("<HH", elf, 0x2A)
    segs = []
    for i in range(phnum):
        p_type, off, vaddr, _, filesz, memsz = struct.unpack_from("<6I", elf, phoff + i * phentsize)
        if p_type == 1:
            segs.append((off, vaddr, filesz, memsz))
    return segs


def irx_info(elf: bytes):
    """(name, version) from an IRX .iopmod section, or None if not an IRX."""
    if elf[:4] != b"\x7fELF" or struct.unpack_from("<H", elf, 0x10)[0] != ET_SCE_IOPRELEXEC:
        return None
    shoff = struct.unpack_from("<I", elf, 0x20)[0]
    shentsize, shnum = struct.unpack_from("<HH", elf, 0x2E)
    for i in range(shnum):
        sh = struct.unpack_from("<10I", elf, shoff + i * shentsize)
        if sh[1] == SHT_SCE_IOPMOD:
            mod = elf[sh[4]:sh[4] + sh[5]]
            ver = struct.unpack_from("<H", mod, 0x18)[0]
            return mod[0x1A:].split(b"\0")[0].decode("ascii", "replace"), f"{ver >> 8}.{ver & 0xFF:02d}"
    return "unknown", "?"


def game_layout(elf: bytes):
    """Slot table and per-slot segment sizes declared by the main ELF."""
    segs = elf_segments(elf)
    off, vaddr = segs[0][0], segs[0][1]
    # The table sits at 0x1C8A30 in the European ELF, 0x1C8AB0 in the American one: found as the main ELF's base
    # followed by its first overlay segment's address.
    at = elf.find(struct.pack("<2I", 0x100000, segs[1][1]))
    if at < 0:
        at = SLOT_TABLE_VADDR - vaddr + off
    table = list(struct.unpack_from(f"<{SLOT_COUNT}I", elf, at))
    declared = defaultdict(list)
    for _, va, _, memsz in segs[1:]:
        declared[va].append(memsz)
    return table, declared


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- chunk discovery

def plausible_header(buf, pos):
    if pos + 16 > len(buf):
        return None
    magic, typ, comp, decomp, nxt = slz.HEADER.unpack_from(buf, pos)
    if magic not in (b"SLZ", b"SLE") or typ > 3:
        return None
    if not (0 < comp <= MAX_COMP and 0 < decomp <= MAX_DECOMP) or comp > decomp + 64:
        return None
    if nxt and not (16 + comp <= nxt <= 16 + comp + MAX_LINK_GAP):
        return None
    return magic, typ, comp, decomp, nxt


def scan_window(args):
    """Find plausible chunk headers in one window; keep those whose start decodes."""
    iso, start = args
    with open(iso, "rb") as f:
        f.seek(start)
        buf = f.read(SPAN + MAX_COMP + 16)
    found = []
    for magic in (b"SLZ", b"SLE"):
        pos = buf.find(magic)
        while 0 <= pos < SPAN:
            h = plausible_header(buf, pos)
            if h:
                try:
                    slz.decompress_chunk(buf, pos, slz.SLE_KEY, peek=64)
                    found.append((start + pos,) + h)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            pos = buf.find(magic, pos + 1)
    return found


def build_chains(headers):
    """Group headers into chains using validated 'next' links; returns list of member offset lists."""
    by_off = {h[0]: h for h in headers}
    linked = {h[0] + h[5] for h in headers if h[5] and h[0] + h[5] in by_off}
    chains = []
    for h in sorted(headers):
        if h[0] in linked:
            continue
        members = [h[0]]
        while by_off[members[-1]][5] and members[-1] + by_off[members[-1]][5] in by_off:
            members.append(members[-1] + by_off[members[-1]][5])
        chains.append(members)
    return chains


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- part classification

def classify(part: bytes, slots: set):
    if len(part) >= 0x80:
        w = struct.unpack_from("<8I", part)
        if w[0] in (0, MWO3) and w[2] in slots:
            return "module", w
    if part[:4] == b"\x7fELF":
        return ("irx" if irx_info(part) else "elf"), None
    if len(part) >= 0x20:
        count = struct.unpack_from("<I", part, 4)[0]
        if struct.unpack_from("<I", part, 0)[0] == 0 and 0 < count <= 4096 and len(part) >= 0x10 + 16 * count:
            tags = [part[0x10 + 16 * k:0x14 + 16 * k] for k in range(min(count, 8))]
            if all(t[0] and all(48 <= b < 91 or b == 0 for b in t) for t in tags):
                return "container", None
    tag = part[:4]
    if len(tag) == 4 and all(32 <= b < 127 for b in tag):
        return "tag:" + tag.decode(), None
    return "data", None


EXT = {"module": "mod", "irx": "irx", "elf": "elf", "container": "ctr", "data": "bin"}


def process_chain(args):
    """Decompress one chain; return per-part metadata plus module/IRX payloads."""
    iso, members, slots, assets_dir = args
    with open(iso, "rb") as f:
        f.seek(members[0])
        head_last = members[-1] - members[0]
        f.seek(members[-1])
        last = slz.parse_header(f.read(16))
        f.seek(members[0])
        buf = f.read(head_last + 16 + last[2])
    parts, blobs = [], []
    for n, m in enumerate(members):
        rel = m - members[0]
        try:
            data = slz.decompress_chunk(buf, rel, slz.SLE_KEY)
        except Exception as e:
            parts.append((n, "error", 0, repr(e)[:60]))
            continue
        kind, w = classify(data, slots)
        parts.append((n, kind, len(data), ""))
        if kind in ("module", "irx"):
            blobs.append((n, kind, data))
        if assets_dir:
            ext = EXT.get(kind, kind[4:].strip().lower() or "bin")
            sub = os.path.join(assets_dir, f"{members[0] >> 28:x}")
            os.makedirs(sub, exist_ok=True)
            with open(os.path.join(sub, f"{members[0]:010x}_{n:03d}.{ext}"), "wb") as o:
                o.write(data)
    return members, parts, blobs


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- main

def safe_name(s):
    return "".join(c if c.isalnum() or c in "._-" else "_" for c in s)


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.split("\n")[0])
    ap.add_argument("iso")
    ap.add_argument("out")
    ap.add_argument("--assets", action="store_true", help="also dump every decompressed part (~2.7 GiB)")
    ap.add_argument("--jobs", type=int, default=os.cpu_count())
    args = ap.parse_args()

    t0 = time.time()
    out = args.out
    for d in ("iso", "modules", "iop"):
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(out, d), exist_ok=True)
    iso_size = os.path.getsize(args.iso)

    # 1. ISO9660 files
    with open(args.iso, "rb") as f:
        files = iso_root_files(f)
        for name, lba, size in files:
            f.seek(lba * SECTOR)
            with open(os.path.join(out, "iso", name), "wb") as o:
                o.write(f.read(size))
    boot = next((n for n, _, _ in files if n.startswith(("SLES", "SLUS", "SLPM", "SLPS"))), None)
    if not boot:
        raise SystemExit("boot executable not found in the ISO root")
    elf = open(os.path.join(out, "iso", boot), "rb").read()
    table, declared = game_layout(elf)
    slots = {a for a in table if a and a != 0x100000}
    print(f"[1/4] ISO filesystem: {len(files)} files, boot {boot}")

    # 2. Chunk headers and chains
    with Pool(args.jobs) as pool:
        headers = [h for part in pool.map(scan_window, [(args.iso, s) for s in range(0, iso_size, SPAN)])
                   for h in part]
    chains = build_chains(headers)
    print(f"[2/4] chunk headers: {len(headers)}, chains: {len(chains)}  ({time.time() - t0:.0f}s)")

    # 3. Decompress every chain, classify parts, collect modules and IRX
    assets_dir = os.path.join(out, "assets") if args.assets else None
    jobs = [(args.iso, c, slots, assets_dir) for c in chains]
    jobs.sort(key=lambda j: j[1][0])
    modules = {}
    irx = {}
    kinds = Counter()
    errors = []
    decompressed = 0
    with open(os.path.join(out, "chunks.tsv"), "w") as idx:
        idx.write("offset\tlba\tparts\tkinds\n")
        with Pool(args.jobs) as pool:
            for members, parts, blobs in pool.imap(process_chain, jobs, chunksize=8):
                for n, kind, size, err in parts:
                    kinds[kind.split(":")[0]] += 1
                    decompressed += size
                    if kind == "error":
                        errors.append((members[n], err))
                idx.write(f"{members[0]}\t{members[0] // SECTOR}\t{len(members)}\t"
                          + ",".join(k for _, k, _, _ in parts) + "\n")
                for n, kind, data in blobs:
                    h = hashlib.sha1(data).hexdigest()
                    where = {"offset": members[n], "chain": members[0], "part": n}
                    store = modules if kind == "module" else irx
                    if h not in store:
                        store[h] = {"data": data, "occurrences": []}
                    store[h]["occurrences"].append(where)
    print(f"[3/4] decompressed {decompressed / 2**30:.2f} GiB, parts by kind {dict(kinds)}, "
          f"errors {len(errors)}  ({time.time() - t0:.0f}s)")

    # 4. Export modules and IRX, validate against the ELF
    mod_meta = []
    valid = 0
    for h, m in sorted(modules.items(), key=lambda kv: struct.unpack_from("<II", kv[1]["data"], 4)[::-1]):
        d = m["data"]
        w = struct.unpack_from("<8I", d)
        name = d[0x20:0x60].split(b"\0")[0].decode("ascii", "replace") if w[0] == MWO3 else ""
        mem_size = len(d) + w[5]
        ok = any((mem_size + 0x7F) & ~0x7F == s or mem_size == s for s in declared.get(w[2], []))
        valid += ok
        fname = f"{w[2]:08x}_{w[1]:03d}" + (f"_{safe_name(os.path.splitext(name)[0])}" if name else "") + ".bin"
        with open(os.path.join(out, "modules", fname), "wb") as o:
            o.write(d)
        mod_meta.append({
            "file": fname, "id": w[1], "name": name or None, "format": "MWo3" if w[0] == MWO3 else "module",
            "load": f"0x{w[2]:08x}", "slots": [i for i, a in enumerate(table) if a == w[2]],
            "code_start": f"0x{w[2] + 0x80:08x}", "text_size": w[3], "data_size": w[4], "bss_size": w[5],
            "ctors": [f"0x{w[6]:08x}", f"0x{w[7]:08x}"], "size": len(d), "sha1": h,
            "matches_elf_segment": ok, "occurrences": m["occurrences"],
        })
    irx_meta = []
    for h, m in irx.items():
        name, ver = irx_info(m["data"])
        fname = f"{safe_name(name)}.irx"
        with open(os.path.join(out, "iop", fname), "wb") as o:
            o.write(m["data"])
        irx_meta.append({"file": fname, "name": name, "version": ver, "size": len(m["data"]), "sha1": h,
                         "occurrences": m["occurrences"]})

    with open(os.path.join(out, "modules.json"), "w") as o:
        json.dump(mod_meta, o, indent=1)
    found_slots = Counter(m["load"] for m in mod_meta)
    slot_report = []
    for i, a in enumerate(table):
        if a and a != 0x100000:
            slot_report.append({"slot": i, "address": f"0x{a:08x}", "declared": len(declared.get(a, [])),
                                "found": found_slots.get(f"0x{a:08x}", 0)})
    manifest = {
        "tool": "VP2S_Recomp tools/extract.py", "version": VERSION,
        "iso": {"path": os.path.basename(args.iso), "size": iso_size, "boot": boot,
                "boot_sha1": hashlib.sha1(elf).hexdigest()},
        "chunk_headers": len(headers), "chains": len(chains), "parts_by_kind": dict(kinds),
        "decompressed_bytes": decompressed, "errors": [{"offset": o, "error": e} for o, e in errors],
        "modules": {"unique": len(mod_meta), "match_elf_segments": valid},
        "iop_modules": sorted(m["name"] for m in irx_meta),
        "slots": slot_report, "assets_dumped": bool(args.assets),
        "seconds": round(time.time() - t0),
    }
    with open(os.path.join(out, "manifest.json"), "w") as o:
        json.dump(manifest, o, indent=1)
    print(f"[4/4] modules: {len(mod_meta)} unique ({valid} match their ELF segment), IOP modules: {len(irx_meta)}")
    for s in slot_report:
        if s["found"] or s["declared"]:
            print(f"      slot {s['slot']:2} {s['address']}: {s['found']:3} found / {s['declared']:3} declared")
    print(f"done in {time.time() - t0:.0f}s -> {out}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/find_entry_points.py`:

```py
"""Find functions reached only through pointers (thread entries, callbacks, vtables).

Ghidra creates functions from direct calls; a routine only ever referenced by a pointer
can be missed, and the recompiled program then has nothing to dispatch to. This scans
each input image for code pointers:
  - aligned words anywhere in the loaded image (data, tables, vtables);
  - lui + addiu/ori pairs in code (e.g. a thread entry passed to CreateThread);
and keeps targets that look like a function start: an `addiu sp,sp,-N` prologue, or the
instruction after the previous function's end (`jr ra`, `j` or `b`, its delay slot and
any zero alignment padding). Every target of a module's constructor table (header
+0x18..+0x1c) is kept as is, and so is every jal from one input into the code of another
(a module into the main ELF or another module, the main ELF into the resident core module):
Ghidra analyzes each input alone and never sees those calls.
The jal of every new entry are then followed, up to its first jr ra, until nothing new
appears. The boot ELF's single section also holds data, so its guessed pointers must land in
the blocks where Ghidra found functions. Pointers into the middle of a function (switch
tables, labels) fail both tests and are ignored, as are addresses Ghidra already knows.

Targets in the input's own code are attributed to it; a target in another input's code goes
to every input loaded at another address that has a function start there (a vtable in the
main ELF can point into the resident core module).
"""
import bisect
import csv
import struct
from pathlib import Path

JR_RA = 0x03E00008


def load_image(elf_path: Path):
    """(load address, image bytes, executable ranges) of a single-image ELF."""
    data = elf_path.read_bytes()
    phoff = struct.unpack_from("<I", data, 0x1C)[0]
    phentsize, phnum = struct.unpack_from("<HH", data, 0x2A)
    shoff = struct.unpack_from("<I", data, 0x20)[0]
    shentsize, shnum = struct.unpack_from("<HH", data, 0x2E)
    load = None
    for i in range(phnum):
        p_type, off, vaddr, _, filesz, _ = struct.unpack_from("<6I", data, phoff + i * phentsize)
        if p_type == 1 and filesz:
            load, image = vaddr, data[off:off + filesz]
            break
    code = []
    for i in range(shnum):
        sh = struct.unpack_from("<10I", data, shoff + i * shentsize)
        if sh[2] & 0x4 and sh[3]:  # SHF_EXECINSTR
            code.append((sh[3], sh[3] + sh[5]))
    if not code:  # no section headers: whole image
        code = [(load, load + len(image))]
    return load, image, code


def known_starts(csv_path: Path) -> set[int]:
    if not csv_path.exists():
        return set()
    with open(csv_path, newline="") as f:
        return {int(row["Start"], 16) for row in csv.DictReader(f)}


class Image:
    def __init__(self, elf_path: Path):
        self.load, self.bytes, self.code = load_image(elf_path)
        self.sections = list(self.code)  # executable sections; self.code may be narrowed

    def in_section(self, addr: int) -> bool:
        return any(lo <= addr < hi for lo, hi in self.sections)

    def in_code(self, addr: int) -> bool:
        return any(lo <= addr < hi for lo, hi in self.code)

    def word(self, addr: int) -> int | None:
        off = addr - self.load
        if 0 <= off <= len(self.bytes) - 4:
            return struct.unpack_from("<I", self.bytes, off)[0]
        return None

    def looks_like_function_start(self, addr: int) -> bool:
        if addr & 3 or not self.in_code(addr):
            return False
        first = self.word(addr)
        if first is not None and first >> 16 == 0x27BD and first & 0x8000:  # addiu sp,sp,-N
            return True
        if self.word(addr - 8) == JR_RA:
            return True
        # Functions are often 16-byte aligned: skip the zero padding (and a nop delay slot)
        # back to the previous function's jr ra.
        back = addr - 4
        while back > addr - 20 and self.word(back) == 0:
            back -= 4
        # The previous function ends with jr ra or with an unconditional jump (j, e.g. a C++
        # this-adjusting thunk, or b = beq zero,zero), followed by its delay slot.
        ends = lambda w: w is not None and (w == JR_RA or w >> 26 == 0x02 or w >> 16 == 0x1000)
        if back == addr - 4 or not (ends(self.word(back)) or ends(self.word(back - 4))):
            return False
        if not first:  # still inside the zero padding
            return False
        # The same layout ends the static initializers before a constructor table: a first
        # word that is an address in the image is a pointer table, not code.
        return not self.load <= (first or 0) < self.load + len(self.bytes)

    def call_targets(self) -> set[int]:
        """Targets of the jal instructions in this image's code (the narrowed code blocks for the
        main ELF, whose single section also holds data)."""
        targets = set()
        for lo, hi in self.code:
            for addr in range(lo, hi, 4):
                w = self.word(addr)
                if w is not None and w >> 26 == 0x03:  # jal
                    targets.add(((addr + 4) & 0xF0000000) | ((w & 0x03FFFFFF) << 2))
        return targets

    def calls_from(self, start: int, limit: int = 0x4000) -> set[int]:
        """jal targets from start up to the first jr ra (a linear scan of one function)."""
        targets = set()
        for addr in range(start, start + limit, 4):
            w = self.word(addr)
            if w is None or not self.in_section(addr):
                break
            if w >> 26 == 0x03:
                targets.add(((addr + 4) & 0xF0000000) | ((w & 0x03FFFFFF) << 2))
            if w == JR_RA:
                break
        return targets

    def pointers(self):
        """Candidate code addresses referenced from this image."""
        words = struct.unpack_from(f"<{len(self.bytes) // 4}I", self.bytes)
        found = set(words)
        for i, w in enumerate(words):
            if w >> 26 != 0x0F or not self.in_code(self.load + i * 4):  # lui in code
                continue
            rt, hi = (w >> 16) & 0x1F, (w & 0xFFFF) << 16
            for nxt in words[i + 1:i + 9]:
                op, rs = nxt >> 26, (nxt >> 21) & 0x1F
                if rs == rt and op in (0x09, 0x0D):  # addiu / ori from the lui register
                    imm = nxt & 0xFFFF
                    found.add((hi + (imm - 0x10000 if op == 0x09 and imm & 0x8000 else imm)) & 0xFFFFFFFF)
                    break
        return found


def code_blocks(csv_path: Path, max_gap: int = 0x400) -> list[tuple[int, int]]:
    """Ranges covered by the functions Ghidra found, merging gaps up to max_gap bytes."""
    if not csv_path.exists():
        return []
    with open(csv_path, newline="") as f:
        ranges = sorted((int(row["Start"], 16), int(row["End"], 16)) for row in csv.DictReader(f))
    blocks: list[list[int]] = []
    for start, end in ranges:
        if blocks and start - blocks[-1][1] <= max_gap:
            blocks[-1][1] = max(blocks[-1][1], end)
        else:
            blocks.append([start, end])
    return [(start, end + max_gap) for start, end in blocks]


def find_entry_points(images: dict[str, Image], csvs: dict[str, Path]) -> dict[str, list[int]]:
    """Entry points per input name; 'main' must be present."""
    main = images["main"]
    known = {name: known_starts(path) for name, path in csvs.items()}
    # The boot ELF has a single section covering code and data (including data between code
    # blocks, and static initializers after the data): its code is where Ghidra found
    # functions, or data words that happen to follow a jr ra or a j would pass for functions.
    blocks = code_blocks(csvs.get("main", Path()))
    if blocks:
        main.code = blocks
    result = {name: set() for name in images}
    for name, image in images.items():
        if name != "main":
            # Static initializers listed by the module header's constructor table (+0x18..+0x1c)
            # are functions whatever their first instruction looks like.
            table_lo, table_hi = image.word(image.load + 0x18) or 0, image.word(image.load + 0x1C) or 0
            for slot in range(table_lo, table_hi if table_hi - table_lo <= 0x1000 else table_lo, 4):
                target = image.word(slot)
                if target and image.in_code(target) and target not in known.get(name, set()):
                    result[name].add(target)
        # C++ this-adjusting thunks (`j method`, `addiu a0,a0,-N` in its delay slot, nop padding to 16
        # bytes): only vtables reach them, and those may be built at run time or live in another input.
        # Modules keep them after their last function, where the section split may have put .data
        # already, so the whole image is scanned (VP2 hung leaving Logun on mod010's thunk at 0x494c60).
        for addr in range(image.load, image.load + len(image.bytes) - 16, 16):
            w = [image.word(addr + 4 * k) for k in range(4)]
            if (w[0] >> 26 == 0x02 and w[1] >> 16 == 0x2484 and w[2] == 0 and w[3] == 0
                    and addr not in known.get(name, set())):
                result[name].add(addr)
        # Routines of another input called only from this one: an overlay calling the main ELF
        # or another module, or the main ELF calling into the resident core module (VP2's main
        # ELF calls 0x248dc0 in module 1). Ghidra analyzes each input alone and never sees those
        # calls. A jal target is a function entry whatever its shape.
        for target in image.call_targets():
            if image.in_section(target):
                continue
            for owner, candidate in images.items():
                if candidate.load != image.load and candidate.in_section(target) and target not in known.get(owner, set()):
                    result[owner].add(target)
        for target in image.pointers():
            if image.looks_like_function_start(target):
                owners = [name]
            else:
                # A pointer into another input's code (e.g. a vtable in the main ELF pointing
                # into the resident core module) goes to every input loaded elsewhere that has
                # a function start there; an extra entry in a module sharing the slot is harmless.
                owners = [other for other, candidate in images.items()
                          if candidate.load != image.load and candidate.looks_like_function_start(target)]
            for owner in owners:
                if target not in known.get(owner, set()):
                    result[owner].add(target)

    # A branch (or j) that crosses a function start lands in the middle of another function as the recompiler
    # splits them, and is emitted as a jump to its target: the target needs an entry (module 27's 0x36b950
    # branches to 0x36ba40, past the entry at 0x36ba10, and the tutorial hung there).
    for name, image in images.items():
        starts = sorted(known.get(name, set()) | result[name])
        if not starts:
            continue
        for lo, hi in image.code:
            for addr in range(lo, hi, 4):
                w = image.word(addr)
                if w is None:
                    continue
                op = w >> 26
                if op in (0x04, 0x05, 0x06, 0x07, 0x14, 0x15, 0x16, 0x17) or (op == 0x01 and ((w >> 16) & 0x1F) in (0, 1, 2, 3, 16, 17, 18, 19)):
                    imm = w & 0xFFFF
                    target = addr + 4 + ((imm - 0x10000 if imm & 0x8000 else imm) << 2)
                elif op == 0x02:
                    target = ((addr + 4) & 0xF0000000) | ((w & 0x03FFFFFF) << 2)
                else:
                    continue
                if not image.in_code(target) or target in known.get(name, set()) or target in result[name]:
                    continue
                low, high = min(addr, target), max(addr, target)
                i = bisect.bisect_right(starts, low)
                if i < len(starts) and starts[i] <= high:
                    result[name].add(target)

    # The two passes below feed each other (a routine found through a jal can cut a switch's function, and a
    # case entry can call routines Ghidra missed): repeat them until neither finds anything new (VP2's mod028
    # froze a battle on a jr to 0x383968, cut off by 0x383940 that the jal pass found after the table pass).
    while True:
        before = sum(len(targets) for targets in result.values())
        # A switch's jump table (consecutive words pointing into the image's code, outside the code) whose
        # targets cross a function start: Ghidra cut the switch's function short, and a jr to a case past
        # the cut lands in the middle of another function, with no entry (VP2's mod003 at 0x4438b8 jumps to
        # 0x4439d0 inside 0x4439b8 after a save is loaded, and the game hung there). Those cases get entries.
        for name, image in images.items():
            starts = sorted(known.get(name, set()) | result[name])
            if not starts:
                continue
            run: list[int] = []
            for addr in range(image.load, image.load + len(image.bytes) - 3, 4):
                w = image.word(addr)
                if w is not None and not w & 3 and image.in_code(w) and not image.in_code(addr):
                    run.append(w)
                    continue
                if len(run) >= 2 and max(run) - min(run) <= 0x1000:
                    containing = lambda t: starts[bisect.bisect_right(starts, t) - 1] if bisect.bisect_right(starts, t) else None
                    first = containing(min(run))
                    for target in run:
                        if (containing(target) != first and target not in known.get(name, set())
                                and target not in result[name]):
                            result[name].add(target)
                run = []

        # Routines Ghidra missed call others it missed too: follow the jal of every new entry, up
        # to its first jr ra, until no new target appears.
        pending = [(name, target) for name, targets in result.items() for target in targets]
        while pending:
            name, start = pending.pop()
            image = images[name]
            for target in image.calls_from(start):
                for owner, candidate in images.items():
                    in_owner = candidate.in_section(target) and (owner == name or candidate.load != image.load)
                    if in_owner and target not in known.get(owner, set()) and target not in result[owner]:
                        result[owner].add(target)
                        pending.append((owner, target))
        if sum(len(targets) for targets in result.values()) == before:
            break

    return {name: sorted(targets) for name, targets in result.items()}

```

`tools/pipeline.py`:

```py
"""Build pipeline: game ISO -> recompiled C++ for the main ELF and every code module.

Stages, in order (each skips the work whose outputs are newer than its inputs):
  extract  tools/extract.py                                    -> build/extract/
  wrap     main ELF copy + tools/wrap_elf.py per module         -> build/elf/
  ghidra   Ghidra headless analysis + PS2Recomp function export -> build/ghidra/export/
  tool     MSVC build of the fork tools (ps2_recomp, ps2_analyzer, ps2_vu_aot) -> build/tests/
  sdk      ps2_analyzer SDK signature matching per input        -> build/sdk/<name>.toml
  entries  functions reached only through pointers              -> build/entries/<name>.txt
  recomp   ps2_recomp per input (Ghidra map + SDK bindings);
           modules in overlay module mode                       -> build/recomp/<name>/
  vu       ps2_vu_aot per input: VU1 microcode compiled ahead
           of time (inputs without microcode get nothing;
           fast mode unless VP2S_VU_EXACT=1)                    -> build/vu_aot/<name>/
  report   per-input and total figures                         -> build/report.json, build/report.md
  port     MSVC build of port/ (runtime + generated code)      -> build/port/vp2s.exe
  test     MSVC build of the fork test suites, then run them    -> build/tests/<suite>.log

Usage (Windows, from the project root):
  python tools/pipeline.py [--from STAGE | --only STAGE] [--force] [--inputs main,mod003]

Everything runs natively on Windows. Requires the Visual Studio 2022 C++ build tools (found
with vswhere, or VP2S_VS_DIR), Ghidra (GHIDRA_INSTALL_DIR, default D:\\Tools\\ghidra_12.1.4_PUBLIC)
and the Emotion Engine extension zip for that Ghidra version (VP2S_GHIDRA_EE_ZIP, default in
D:\\Tools\\downloads). toolchain/Dockerfile remains for Linux builds of the fork, not needed here.
"""
import argparse
import getpass
import hashlib
import json
import zipfile
import os
import re
import shutil
import struct
import subprocess
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
# VP2S_BUILD=<dir under the project root>: another build tree (e.g. build_usa for the USA disc), VP2S_ISO=<name or
# path>: the disc to extract (else the first .iso in the project root).
BUILD = ROOT / os.environ.get("VP2S_BUILD", "build")
EXTRACT = BUILD / "extract"
ELF_DIR = BUILD / "elf"
CAPTURED = BUILD / "captured_modules"
GHIDRA_DIR = BUILD / "ghidra"
EXPORT_DIR = GHIDRA_DIR / "export"
# VP2S_FORK / VP2S_TEST_BUILD / VP2S_RECOMP / VP2S_PORT_BUILD build another fork checkout (e.g. a
# git worktree) into its own build directories, without touching the default ones.
RECOMP_DIR = BUILD / os.environ.get("VP2S_RECOMP", "recomp")
# ps2_recomp writes here first; only changed files are then copied to RECOMP_DIR.
STAGING_DIR = BUILD / f"{RECOMP_DIR.name}-staging"
FORK = Path(os.environ.get("VP2S_FORK", ROOT / "external" / "PS2Recomp"))
# The fork tools and its test suites share one MSVC build of the fork.
TEST_BUILD = BUILD / os.environ.get("VP2S_TEST_BUILD", "tests")
TOOL = TEST_BUILD / "ps2xRecomp" / "ps2_recomp.exe"
ANALYZER = TEST_BUILD / "ps2xAnalyzer" / "ps2_analyzer.exe"
VU_AOT_TOOL = TEST_BUILD / "ps2xRecomp" / "ps2_vu_aot.exe"
VU_AOT_DIR = BUILD / os.environ.get("VP2S_VU_AOT", "vu_aot")
# VU1 microcode on its blocks' static schedule with relaxed floats (ps2_vu_aot, PS2X_VU_AOT_FAST): VU1 about 25%
