```py
# faster, the same GS packets on recorded field runs. VP2S_VU_EXACT=1 keeps the exact pipeline model.
VU_AOT_MODE = "exact" if os.environ.get("VP2S_VU_EXACT") else "fast"
# Fragments kept exact in fast mode: the field lights (mod001 pc 0x6e0..) read Q before a DIV lands, across subroutine
# calls, which the fast static schedule gets wrong (bands across the forest every 128 rows).
VU_AOT_EXACT = "208b5f5ec34b392f"
SDK_DIR = BUILD / "sdk"
ENTRIES_DIR = BUILD / "entries"
# Optional offline cache of CMake dependency sources (<name>-src directories, e.g. copied
# from a previous PS2Recomp build's _deps); dependencies are downloaded when absent.
DEPS_CACHE = BUILD / "deps"
GHIDRA = Path(os.environ.get("GHIDRA_INSTALL_DIR", r"D:\Tools\ghidra_12.1.4_PUBLIC"))
EE_ZIP = Path(os.environ.get("VP2S_GHIDRA_EE_ZIP", r"D:\Tools\downloads\ghidra-emotionengine-reloaded_12.1.4.zip"))
GHIDRA_SETTINGS = GHIDRA_DIR / "settings"
PORT_BUILD = BUILD / os.environ.get("VP2S_PORT_BUILD", "port")
STAGES = ["extract", "wrap", "ghidra", "tool", "sdk", "entries", "recomp", "vu", "report", "port", "test"]
# Roles of the modules without an original file name, inferred during the audit (docs/AUDIT.md).
INFERRED_ROLES = {1: "resident core (inferred)", 2: "title / memory card (inferred)", 3: "field engine (inferred)"}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- helpers

def hpath(path: Path) -> str:
    """Absolute path with forward slashes, as written into the recompiler configs."""
    return path.resolve().as_posix()


def run_logged(cmd: list[str], log: Path) -> None:
    with open(log, "w", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as out:
        result = subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=out, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
    if result.returncode != 0:
        raise SystemExit(f"{Path(cmd[0]).name} failed ({result.returncode}) - see {log}")


def mtime(path: Path) -> float:
    return path.stat().st_mtime if path.exists() else 0.0


def stale(outputs: list[Path], inputs: list[Path], force: bool) -> bool:
    if force or not all(p.exists() for p in outputs):
        return True
    return min(map(mtime, outputs)) < max(map(mtime, inputs), default=0.0)


def modules() -> list[dict]:
    """The extracted modules, then those captured from memory at run time (build/captured_modules,
    see captured_modules)."""
    found = json.loads((EXTRACT / "modules.json").read_text())
    known = {m["id"] for m in found}
    return found + [m for m in captured_modules() if m["id"] not in known]


def captured_modules() -> list[dict]:
    """Modules the game loads that the extractor never found on the disc (docs/AUDIT.md: slots declared
    but not found, e.g. the tutorial's module 27). The VP2S diagnostics save each unknown module they see in
    a slot as build/port/vp2s_module_<load>_<hash>.bin (header included, extractor format); copied into
    build/captured_modules they are wrapped and recompiled like the others."""
    result = []
    for path in sorted(CAPTURED.glob("*.bin")):
        data = path.read_bytes()
        _, ident, load, text, dsize, bss, ctor_lo, ctor_hi = struct.unpack_from("<8I", data, 0)
        ctors = [struct.unpack_from("<I", data, a - load)[0] for a in range(ctor_lo, ctor_hi, 4)
                 if 0 <= a - load <= len(data) - 4] if ctor_hi - ctor_lo <= 0x1000 else []
        result.append({"file": str(path), "id": ident, "name": None, "format": "module", "load": f"0x{load:08x}",
                       "code_start": f"0x{load + 0x80:08x}", "text_size": text - 0x80, "data_size": dsize,
                       "bss_size": bss, "ctors": [f"0x{c:08x}" for c in ctors], "size": len(data),
                       "sha1": hashlib.sha1(data).hexdigest(), "captured": True})
    return result


def module_name(module: dict) -> str:
    return f"mod{module['id']:03d}"


def input_names(selection: set[str] | None) -> list[str]:
    names = ["main"] + [module_name(m) for m in modules()]
    return [n for n in names if selection is None or n in selection]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- stages

def stage_extract(args) -> None:
    chosen = os.environ.get("VP2S_ISO")
    isos = [ROOT / chosen] if chosen else sorted(ROOT.glob("*.iso"))
    if not isos:
        raise SystemExit("no .iso in the project root")
    inputs = [isos[0], ROOT / "tools" / "extract.py", ROOT / "tools" / "slz.py"]
    if not stale([EXTRACT / "manifest.json"], inputs, args.force):
        print("  up to date")
        return
    run_logged([sys.executable, "-I", str(ROOT / "tools" / "extract.py"), str(isos[0]), str(EXTRACT)],
               BUILD / "extract.log")


def stage_wrap(args) -> None:
    ELF_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    boot = json.loads((EXTRACT / "manifest.json").read_text())["iso"]["boot"]
    main_src, main_elf = EXTRACT / "iso" / boot, ELF_DIR / "main.elf"
    if stale([main_elf], [main_src], args.force):
        shutil.copyfile(main_src, main_elf)

    from wrap_elf import wrap

    wrapper = ROOT / "tools" / "wrap_elf.py"
    flags = struct.unpack_from("<I", main_src.read_bytes(), 0x24)[0]
    wrapped = 0
    for module in modules():
        src, dst = EXTRACT / "modules" / module["file"], ELF_DIR / f"{module_name(module)}.elf"
        if stale([dst], [src, wrapper], args.force):
            # Rewrite only on change so unchanged modules are not reanalyzed downstream.
            elf = wrap(src.read_bytes(), flags)
            if not dst.exists() or dst.read_bytes() != elf:
                dst.write_bytes(elf)
                wrapped += 1
    print(f"  {wrapped} module(s) wrapped, {len(modules()) - wrapped} up to date")


def ghidra_env() -> dict:
    """Environment giving Ghidra a settings directory under build/ with the EE extension.

    Python started through the Windows app alias (Store / Python install manager) runs with
    %APPDATA% redirected, so a Ghidra child would not see the user's installed extensions and
    would fall back to a generic MIPS64 language. XDG_CONFIG_HOME avoids %APPDATA% entirely.
    """
    if not EE_ZIP.is_file():
        raise SystemExit(f"Emotion Engine extension zip not found: {EE_ZIP} (set VP2S_GHIDRA_EE_ZIP)")
    # Ghidra names the directory "<user>-ghidra" outside the home directory; fill both forms.
    for app in ("ghidra", f"{getpass.getuser()}-ghidra"):
        extensions = GHIDRA_SETTINGS / app / GHIDRA.name / "Extensions"
        if not (extensions / "ghidra-emotionengine-reloaded").is_dir():
            extensions.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            with zipfile.ZipFile(EE_ZIP) as archive:
                archive.extractall(extensions)
    return dict(os.environ, XDG_CONFIG_HOME=str(GHIDRA_SETTINGS))


def stage_ghidra(args) -> None:
    EXPORT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    scripts = GHIDRA_DIR / "scripts"
    scripts.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    # analyzeHeadless.bat splits arguments on ';', so both scripts share one directory.
    shutil.copy(ROOT / "ghidra_scripts" / "PipelineExport.java", scripts)
    shutil.copy(FORK / "ps2xRecomp" / "tools" / "ghidra" / "ExportPS2Functions.java", scripts)

    todo = []
    for name in input_names(args.inputs):
        elf = ELF_DIR / f"{name}.elf"
        outs = [EXPORT_DIR / f"{name}.toml", EXPORT_DIR / f"{name}_functions.csv"]
        if stale(outs, [elf], args.force):
            todo.append(elf)
    if not todo:
        print("  up to date")
        return

    project = GHIDRA_DIR / "project"
    project.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    # Force the Emotion Engine language: auto-detection can pick a generic MIPS64 language
    # for the wrapped modules, which silently ruins the analysis.
    cmd = [str(GHIDRA / "support" / "analyzeHeadless.bat"), str(project), "pipeline",
           "-import", *map(str, todo), "-overwrite", "-processor", "r5900:LE:32:default",
           "-scriptPath", str(scripts), "-postScript", "PipelineExport.java", str(EXPORT_DIR)]
    print(f"  analyzing {len(todo)} ELF(s) in one Ghidra session (log: build/ghidra/ghidra.log)")
    env = ghidra_env()
    with open(GHIDRA_DIR / "ghidra.log", "w", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as log:
        result = subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, env=env)
    missing = [e.stem for e in todo if not (EXPORT_DIR / f"{e.stem}.toml").exists()]
    if result.returncode != 0 or missing:
        raise SystemExit(f"Ghidra export failed (exit {result.returncode}, missing: {missing}) "
                         "- see build/ghidra/ghidra.log")
    languages = set(re.findall(r"Using Language/Compiler: (\S+)",
                               (GHIDRA_DIR / "ghidra.log").read_text(errors="replace")))
    if languages != {"r5900:LE:32:default:default"}:
        raise SystemExit(f"unexpected Ghidra language(s) {sorted(languages)} - see build/ghidra/ghidra.log")


def stage_tool(args) -> None:
    sources = [p for d in ("ps2xRecomp/src", "ps2xRecomp/include", "ps2xAnalyzer/src", "ps2xAnalyzer/include")
               for p in (FORK / d).rglob("*") if p.is_file()]
    # ps2_vu_aot plans its blocks with the runtime's VU1 headers.
    sources += list((FORK / "ps2xRuntime" / "include" / "runtime").glob("ps2_vu1*.h"))
    if not stale([TOOL, ANALYZER, VU_AOT_TOOL], sources, args.force):
        print("  up to date")
        return
    configure_fork_build(args.force)
    run_msvc([f'cmake --build "{TEST_BUILD.as_posix()}" --target ps2_recomp ps2_analyzer ps2_vu_aot'],
             TEST_BUILD / "tools.log")


def stage_sdk(args) -> None:
    """Match Sony SDK functions by signature so they bind to the runtime's HLE handlers."""
    SDK_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    analyzed = 0
    for name in input_names(args.inputs):
        elf, out = ELF_DIR / f"{name}.elf", SDK_DIR / f"{name}.toml"
        if stale([out], [elf, ANALYZER], args.force):
            run_logged([str(ANALYZER), str(elf), str(out)], SDK_DIR / f"{name}.log")
            analyzed += 1
    total = sum(len(sdk_bindings(SDK_DIR / f"{n}.toml")) for n in input_names(args.inputs))
    print(f"  {analyzed} input(s) analyzed; {total} SDK bindings in total")


def stage_entries(args) -> None:
    """Functions reached only through pointers become recompiler entry points."""
    from find_entry_points import Image, find_entry_points

    names = input_names(None)  # main is always needed to attribute cross-references
    inputs = [ELF_DIR / f"{n}.elf" for n in names] + [EXPORT_DIR / f"{n}_functions.csv" for n in names]
    outputs = [ENTRIES_DIR / f"{n}.txt" for n in names]
    inputs.append(ROOT / "tools" / "find_entry_points.py")
    if not stale(outputs, inputs, args.force):
        print("  up to date")
        return
    ENTRIES_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    images = {n: Image(ELF_DIR / f"{n}.elf") for n in names}
    found = find_entry_points(images, {n: EXPORT_DIR / f"{n}_functions.csv" for n in names})
    for name in names:
        # Rewrite only on change so unchanged inputs are not recompiled downstream.
        path, text = ENTRIES_DIR / f"{name}.txt", "".join(f"0x{a:08x}\n" for a in found[name])
        if not path.exists() or path.read_text() != text:
            path.write_text(text)
        else:
            path.touch()
    print(f"  {sum(map(len, found.values()))} entry points ({len(found['main'])} in the main ELF)")


def entry_points(name: str) -> list[str]:
    path = ENTRIES_DIR / f"{name}.txt"
    return path.read_text().split() if path.exists() else []


STUB_ENTRY = re.compile(r'"([^"@]+)@(0x[0-9A-Fa-f]+)"')


def stub_block(text: str) -> re.Match | None:
    return re.search(r"(?ms)^stubs = \[(.*?)^\]", text)


def sdk_bindings(path: Path) -> dict[int, str]:
    """name@address stub bindings of a TOML file, keyed by address."""
    if not path.exists():
        return {}
    block = stub_block(path.read_text())
    if not block:
        return {}
    return {int(addr, 16): name for name, addr in STUB_ENTRY.findall(block.group(1))}


# SDK functions the signature base does not match, identified by hand. The runtime's HLE
# handler named here replaces the recompiled guest code at that address.
MANUAL_BINDINGS: dict[str, dict[int, str]] = {
    "main": {
        # sceSifLoadModuleBuffer(addr, args, argp): calls _sceSifLoadModuleBuffer with a
        # result slot on its stack. Loads the IRX modules copied to IOP memory at boot.
        0x001400F8: "sceSifLoadModuleBuffer",
    },
    "mod001": {
        # libmpeg accessors of mp->sys (pointer at +0x40) next to the matched libmpeg functions:
        # the runtime emulates libmpeg with its own state, so they must use it too.
        0x001F0438: "sceMpegIsEnd",
        0x001F0448: "sceMpegIsRefBuffEmpty",
        0x001F0458: "sceMpegSetDecodeMode",
    },
}


def write_config(name: str) -> Path:
    """Export TOML rewritten with container paths; modules get overlay module mode."""
    text = (EXPORT_DIR / f"{name}.toml").read_text()
    replacements = {
        "input": hpath(ELF_DIR / f"{name}.elf"),
        "output": hpath(STAGING_DIR / name),
        "ghidra_output": hpath(EXPORT_DIR / f"{name}_functions.csv"),
    }
    for key, value in replacements.items():
        text = re.sub(rf'^{key} = .*$', f'{key} = "{value}"', text, count=1, flags=re.M)

    # SDK functions matched by signature win over Ghidra's names at the same address.
    stubs = sdk_bindings(EXPORT_DIR / f"{name}.toml") | sdk_bindings(SDK_DIR / f"{name}.toml")
    stubs |= MANUAL_BINDINGS.get(name, {})
    entries = "".join(f'  "{stubs[a]}@0x{a:08X}",\n' for a in sorted(stubs))
    block = stub_block(text)
    if block:
        text = text[:block.start(1)] + "\n" + entries + text[block.end(1):]
    elif stubs:
        text = text.replace("[general]\n", f"[general]\nstubs = [\n{entries}]\n", 1)

    hints = entry_points(name)
    if hints:
        listed = "".join(f'  "{h}",\n' for h in hints)
        text = text.replace("[general]\n", f"[general]\nentry_points = [\n{listed}]\n", 1)
    if name != "main":
        text = text.replace("[general]\n", f'[general]\nmodule_name = "{name}"\n', 1)
    config = RECOMP_DIR / "config" / f"{name}.toml"
    config.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    # Rewrite only on change: the config's date tells stage_recomp whether to regenerate.
    if not config.exists() or config.read_text() != text:
        config.write_text(text)
    return config


def stage_recomp(args) -> None:
    RECOMP_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    done = 0
    for name in input_names(args.inputs):
        stamp = RECOMP_DIR / name / ".done"
        config = write_config(name)
        inputs = [config, EXPORT_DIR / f"{name}_functions.csv", TOOL]
        if not stale([stamp], inputs, args.force):
            continue
        staging = STAGING_DIR / name
        shutil.rmtree(staging, ignore_errors=True)
        start = time.time()
        run_logged([str(TOOL), str(config)], RECOMP_DIR / f"{name}.log")
        changed, stats = sync_generated(staging, RECOMP_DIR / name)
        shutil.rmtree(staging, ignore_errors=True)
        (RECOMP_DIR / name / ".stats").write_text(json.dumps(stats))
        stamp.write_text(f"{time.time() - start:.1f}\n")
        done += 1
        print(f"  {name}: {time.time() - start:.0f}s, {changed} file(s) changed")
    print(f"  {done} input(s) recompiled")


def stage_vu(args) -> None:
    """Compile each input's VU1 microcode (VIF MPG uploads in its data) ahead of time.

    The port links the generated fragments; at run time one replaces the VU1 interpreter only
    while VU1 code memory holds its exact bytes, so stray matches in the scan are harmless.
    """
    VU_AOT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for name in input_names(args.inputs):
        out = VU_AOT_DIR / name
        stamp = VU_AOT_DIR / f".{name}.done"  # also for inputs without microcode
        same_mode = stamp.exists() and stamp.read_text().startswith(f"mode={VU_AOT_MODE} exact={VU_AOT_EXACT}\n")
        if same_mode and not stale([stamp], [ELF_DIR / f"{name}.elf", VU_AOT_TOOL], args.force):
            continue
        staging = VU_AOT_DIR / f".{name}-staging"
        shutil.rmtree(staging, ignore_errors=True)
        staging.mkdir(parents=True)
        env = {key: value for key, value in os.environ.items() if key != "PS2X_VU_AOT_FAST"}
        if VU_AOT_MODE == "fast":
            env["PS2X_VU_AOT_FAST"] = "1"
            env["PS2X_VU_AOT_EXACT"] = VU_AOT_EXACT
        result = subprocess.run([str(VU_AOT_TOOL), str(ELF_DIR / f"{name}.elf"), str(staging), f"{name}_"],
                                capture_output=True, text=True, env=env)
        if result.returncode != 0:
            raise SystemExit(f"ps2_vu_aot failed on {name}: {result.stderr.strip()}")
        if any(staging.glob("*.cpp")):
            changed, stats = sync_generated(staging, out)
            print(f"  {name}: {stats['cpp_files']} fragment file(s), {changed} changed")
        else:
            shutil.rmtree(out, ignore_errors=True)
        shutil.rmtree(staging, ignore_errors=True)
        stamp.write_text(f"mode={VU_AOT_MODE} exact={VU_AOT_EXACT}\n" + result.stdout.strip() + "\n")


def sync_generated(source: Path, target: Path) -> tuple[int, dict]:
    """Mirror source into target, touching only files whose content changed.

    Unchanged generated files keep their timestamps, so the MSVC build does not recompile
    them (nor the unity groups that include them). Also returns the report figures, counted
    while the files are in memory anyway.
    """
    target.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    fresh = {p.relative_to(source) for p in source.rglob("*") if p.is_file()}
    changed = 0
    stats = {"cpp_files": 0, "cpp_lines": 0, "bytes": 0}
    for rel in fresh:
        src, dst = source / rel, target / rel
        data = src.read_bytes()
        stats["bytes"] += len(data)
        if rel.suffix == ".cpp":
            stats["cpp_files"] += 1
            stats["cpp_lines"] += data.count(b"\n")
        if not dst.exists() or dst.read_bytes() != data:
            dst.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            dst.write_bytes(data)
            changed += 1
    for old in [p for p in target.rglob("*") if p.is_file()]:
        rel = old.relative_to(target)
        if rel not in fresh and rel.name not in (".done", ".stats"):
            old.unlink()
            changed += 1
    return changed, stats


def stage_report(args) -> None:
    rows = []
    module_info = {module_name(m): m for m in modules()}
    for name in input_names(None):
        log = RECOMP_DIR / f"{name}.log"
        if not log.exists():
            continue
        text = log.read_text(errors="replace")
        counts = re.search(r"Warnings: (\d+), errors: (\d+)", text)
        export = (EXPORT_DIR / f"{name}.toml").read_text()
        functions = re.search(r"function_count = (\d+)", export)
        out = RECOMP_DIR / name
        stats_file = out / ".stats"
        if stats_file.exists():  # written by stage_recomp
            stats = json.loads(stats_file.read_text())
        else:
            cpp = list(out.glob("*.cpp"))
            stats = {"cpp_files": len(cpp), "cpp_lines": sum(p.read_bytes().count(b"\n") for p in cpp),
                     "bytes": sum(p.stat().st_size for p in out.iterdir() if p.is_file())}
        info = module_info.get(name, {})
        rows.append({
            "input": name,
            "original": info.get("name") or INFERRED_ROLES.get(info.get("id"), "SLES main ELF" if name == "main" else ""),
            "load": info.get("load", "0x00100000"),
            "functions": int(functions.group(1)) if functions else None,
            "ok": "Recompilation completed successfully" in text,
            "warnings": int(counts.group(1)) if counts else None,
            "errors": int(counts.group(2)) if counts else None,
            "cpp_files": stats["cpp_files"],
            "cpp_lines": stats["cpp_lines"],
            "megabytes": round(stats["bytes"] / 2**20, 1),
            "seconds": float((out / ".done").read_text()) if (out / ".done").exists() else None,
        })
    totals = {k: sum(r[k] or 0 for r in rows) for k in ("functions", "warnings", "errors", "cpp_files", "cpp_lines", "megabytes")}
    totals["inputs"], totals["ok"] = len(rows), sum(r["ok"] for r in rows)
    (BUILD / "report.json").write_text(json.dumps({"inputs": rows, "totals": totals}, indent=1))

    md = ["# Pipeline report", "",
          "| Input | Original | Load | Functions | OK | Warnings | Errors | C++ files | C++ lines | MB | s |",
          "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|"]
    for r in rows:
        md.append(f"| {r['input']} | {r['original']} | {r['load']} | {r['functions']} | {'yes' if r['ok'] else 'NO'} "
                  f"| {r['warnings']} | {r['errors']} | {r['cpp_files']} | {r['cpp_lines']:,} | {r['megabytes']} "
                  f"| {r['seconds'] or ''} |")
    md.append(f"| **total** | {totals['inputs']} inputs, {totals['ok']} ok | | {totals['functions']} | "
              f"| {totals['warnings']} | {totals['errors']} | {totals['cpp_files']} | {totals['cpp_lines']:,} "
              f"| {totals['megabytes']:.1f} | |")
    (BUILD / "report.md").write_text("\n".join(md) + "\n")
    print(f"  {totals['ok']}/{totals['inputs']} inputs recompiled, {totals['functions']} functions, "
          f"{totals['cpp_lines']:,} lines of C++, {totals['errors']} errors -> build/report.md")


def vs_install_dir() -> Path:
    if os.environ.get("VP2S_VS_DIR"):
        return Path(os.environ["VP2S_VS_DIR"])
    vswhere = (Path(os.environ.get("ProgramFiles(x86)", r"C:\Program Files (x86)"))
               / "Microsoft Visual Studio" / "Installer" / "vswhere.exe")
    found = subprocess.run([str(vswhere), "-latest", "-products", "*",
                            "-requires", "Microsoft.VisualStudio.Component.VC.Tools.x86.x64",
                            "-property", "installationPath"], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
    if not found:
        raise SystemExit("Visual Studio 2022 C++ build tools not found (set VP2S_VS_DIR)")
    return Path(found.splitlines()[0])


def run_msvc(commands: list[str], log: Path) -> None:
    """Run command lines in a cmd session set up by vcvars64, with VS's CMake and Ninja first on PATH."""
    vs = vs_install_dir()
    tools = vs / "Common7" / "IDE" / "CommonExtensions" / "Microsoft" / "CMake"
    script = log.with_suffix(".cmd")
    lines = ["@echo off",
             f'call "{vs}\\VC\\Auxiliary\\Build\\vcvars64.bat" >nul || exit /b 1',
             f'set "PATH={tools}\\CMake\\bin;{tools}\\Ninja;%PATH%"']
    lines += [f"{command} || exit /b 1" for command in commands]
    script.write_text("\r\n".join(lines) + "\r\n")
    with open(log, "w", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as out:
        result = subprocess.run(["cmd", "/c", str(script)], stdout=out, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
    if result.returncode != 0:
        raise SystemExit(f"MSVC step failed ({result.returncode}) - see {log}")


def deps_args() -> str:
    """FetchContent overrides pointing at the dependency source cache (no network needed)."""
    if not DEPS_CACHE.is_dir():
        return ""
    return " ".join(f'"-DFETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_{src.name[:-4].upper()}={src.as_posix()}"'
                    for src in DEPS_CACHE.glob("*-src"))


def stage_port(args) -> None:
    PORT_BUILD.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if args.force or not (PORT_BUILD / "CMakeCache.txt").exists():
        print(f"  configuring (log: {(PORT_BUILD / 'configure.log').relative_to(ROOT)})")
        # Reuse an FFmpeg archive another port build already downloaded.
        archives = sorted(BUILD.glob("*/ThirdParty/ffmpeg-prefix/src/ffmpeg-*.zip"))
        ffmpeg = f'"-DFFMPEG_PREBUILT_URL={archives[0].as_posix()}" ' if archives else ""
        run_msvc([f'cmake -S "{(ROOT / "port").as_posix()}" -B "{PORT_BUILD.as_posix()}" -G Ninja '
                  f'-DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release "-DVP2S_FORK={FORK.as_posix()}" '
                  f'"-DVP2S_RECOMP_DIR={RECOMP_DIR.as_posix()}" "-DVP2S_VU_AOT_DIR={VU_AOT_DIR.as_posix()}" '
                  f'{ffmpeg}{deps_args()}'], PORT_BUILD / "configure.log")
    print(f"  building vp2s.exe (log: {(PORT_BUILD / 'build.log').relative_to(ROOT)})", flush=True)
    run_msvc([f'cmake --build "{PORT_BUILD.as_posix()}" --target vp2s'], PORT_BUILD / "build.log")
    exe = PORT_BUILD / "vp2s.exe"
    print(f"  {exe.relative_to(ROOT)}: {exe.stat().st_size / 2**20:.1f} MB")


TEST_TARGETS = ["ps2x_tests", "ps2_iop_emulator_tests", "ps2_iop_import_tests",
                "ps2_iop_compatibility_tests", "ps2_iop_import_version_tests", "ps2_iop_sio2_tests",
                "ps2_iop_spu2_tests"]


def configure_fork_build(force: bool) -> None:
    """MSVC build of the whole fork: recompiler tools, runtime and test suites."""
    TEST_BUILD.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if force or not (TEST_BUILD / "CMakeCache.txt").exists():
        print("  configuring (log: build/tests/configure.log)")
        run_msvc([f'cmake -S "{FORK.as_posix()}" -B "{TEST_BUILD.as_posix()}" -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release '
                  "-DPS2X_BUILD_STUDIO=OFF -DPS2X_ENABLE_FFMPEG=OFF -DPS2X_ENABLE_DEBUG_UI=OFF "
                  "-DPS2X_ENABLE_SCCACHE=OFF -DPS2X_ENABLE_AGRESSIVE_LOGS=OFF -DPS2X_IOP_BUILD_TESTS=ON "
                  f"{deps_args()}"], TEST_BUILD / "configure.log")


def stage_test(args) -> None:
    """Build the fork's test suites with MSVC and run them."""
    configure_fork_build(args.force)
    print("  building (log: build/tests/build.log)", flush=True)
    # A fork checkout (worktree) may predate some suites: only build the ones it defines.
    cmake_text = "".join(f.read_text(errors="replace") for f in FORK.glob("*/CMakeLists.txt"))
    targets = [t for t in TEST_TARGETS if t in cmake_text]
    run_msvc([f'cmake --build "{TEST_BUILD.as_posix()}" --target {" ".join(targets)}'], TEST_BUILD / "build.log")
    failed = []
    for target in targets:
        exe = next(TEST_BUILD.rglob(f"{target}.exe"))
        log = TEST_BUILD / f"{target}.log"
        with open(log, "w", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as out:
            # Some tests read fork sources by relative path: run from the fork root.
            code = subprocess.run([str(exe)], cwd=FORK, stdout=out, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT).returncode
        summary = re.findall(r"^(?:Total Tests|Failed): \d+", log.read_text(errors="replace"), flags=re.M)
        print(f"  {target}: exit {code}" + (f" ({', '.join(summary)})" if summary else ""))
        if code != 0:
            failed.append(target)
    if failed:
        raise SystemExit(f"failing test suites: {', '.join(failed)} - see build/tests/<suite>.log")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- main

def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.split("\n")[0])
    group = ap.add_mutually_exclusive_group()
    group.add_argument("--from", dest="start", choices=STAGES, help="run from this stage on")
    group.add_argument("--only", choices=STAGES, help="run a single stage")
    ap.add_argument("--force", action="store_true", help="redo work even when outputs are up to date")
    ap.add_argument("--inputs", help="comma-separated subset for ghidra/recomp, e.g. main,mod003")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    args.inputs = set(args.inputs.split(",")) if args.inputs else None

    stages = [args.only] if args.only else STAGES[STAGES.index(args.start or "extract"):]
    BUILD.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    for stage in stages:
        start = time.time()
        print(f"[{stage}]", flush=True)
        globals()[f"stage_{stage}"](args)
        print(f"  ({time.time() - start:.0f}s)", flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/region_map.py`:

```py
"""Map addresses of the European build (SLES-54645) to the American one (SLUS-21452).

    python tools/region_map.py [--eu build] [--us build_usa] <address or file> ...

Addresses are looked up in every wrapped ELF of the European tree (build/elf: main.elf, modNNN.elf) that holds them,
then found in the American ELF of the same name. Code: the instructions from the address on, with the fields a
relocation or a moved global changes masked (the immediate of non-register instructions, the target of j / jal), as
long a window as it takes to match once. Data (an address with no code there or no code match): the code that loads
it (lui / addiu or lui / load-store pairs) mapped as code, the address read back from the American instructions.

A file argument: every 0x00XXXXXX constant in it is mapped. Prints "eu -> us (module, how)".
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import re
import struct
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent


class Elf:
    def __init__(self, path: Path):
        self.name = path.stem
        data = path.read_bytes()
        phoff = struct.unpack_from("<I", data, 0x1C)[0]
        count = struct.unpack_from("<H", data, 0x2C)[0]
        self.segments = []  # (vaddr, bytes)
        self.ranges = []  # (vaddr, memsz): bss included
        for i in range(count):
            kind, off, va, _, filesz, memsz = struct.unpack_from("<6I", data, phoff + 32 * i)[:6]
            if kind == 1 and filesz:
                self.segments.append((va, data[off:off + filesz]))
            if kind == 1 and memsz:
                self.ranges.append((va, memsz))

    def holds(self, address: int) -> bool:
        return any(va <= address < va + size for va, size in self.ranges)

    def word(self, address: int) -> int | None:
        for va, b in self.segments:
            if va <= address and address + 4 <= va + len(b):
                return struct.unpack_from("<I", b, address - va)[0]
        return None

    def words(self):
        for va, b in self.segments:
            for k in range(0, len(b) - 3, 4):
                yield va + k, struct.unpack_from("<I", b, k)[0]


def mask(word: int) -> int:
    op = word >> 26
    if op == 0:
        return word
    if op in (2, 3):
        return word & 0xFC000000
    return word & 0xFFFF0000


def looks_like_code(elf: Elf, address: int) -> bool:
    w = [elf.word(address + 4 * k) for k in range(4)]
    return all(x is not None for x in w) and any(x != 0 for x in w) and not all((x >> 24) in (0x3F, 0x3E, 0xBF, 0xBE, 0x00) and x & 0xFFFF == 0 for x in w)


def map_code(eu: Elf, us: Elf, address: int, us_index: dict) -> int | None:
    for length in (6, 10, 16, 24, 40):
        pattern = []
        for k in range(length):
            w = eu.word(address + 4 * k)
            if w is None:
                break
            pattern.append(mask(w))
        if len(pattern) < 4:
            return None
        hits = [a for a in us_index.get(tuple(pattern[:2]), []) if all(
            (w := us.word(a + 4 * k)) is not None and mask(w) == pattern[k] for k in range(len(pattern)))]
        if len(hits) == 1:
            return hits[0]
        if not hits:
            return None
        # several: the one nearest the European address
        if length == 40:
            return min(hits, key=lambda a: abs(a - address))
    return None


def map_data(eu: Elf, us: Elf, address: int, us_index: dict) -> int | None:
    """The data address from the code that builds it: lui hi(address) then an instruction adding lo(address)."""
    hi, lo = (address + 0x8000) >> 16, address & 0xFFFF
    for at, w in eu.words():
        if w >> 26 != 0x0F or (w & 0xFFFF) != hi:
            continue
        reg = (w >> 16) & 31
        for k in range(1, 8):
            n = eu.word(at + 4 * k)
            if n is None or (n >> 26) == 0 or ((n >> 21) & 31) != reg or (n & 0xFFFF) != lo:
                continue
            start = at - 8
            mapped = map_code(eu, us, start, us_index)
            if mapped is None:
                continue
            uw, un = us.word(mapped + 8), us.word(mapped + 8 + 4 * k)
            if uw is None or un is None or uw >> 26 != 0x0F:
                continue
            return ((uw & 0xFFFF) << 16) + struct.unpack("<h", struct.pack("<H", un & 0xFFFF))[0]
    return None


def index(elf: Elf) -> dict:
    out: dict = {}
    prev = None
    for at, w in elf.words():
        if prev is not None:
            out.setdefault((mask(prev[1]), mask(w)), []).append(prev[0])
        prev = (at, w)
    return out


def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
    ap.add_argument("--eu", default="build")
    ap.add_argument("--us", default="build_usa")
    ap.add_argument("items", nargs="+")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    addresses: list[int] = []
    for item in args.items:
        if Path(item).is_file():
            addresses += [int(m, 16) for m in re.findall(r"0x(?:00)?([1-5][0-9A-Fa-f]{5})(?![0-9A-Fa-f])", Path(item).read_text(errors="replace"))]
        else:
            addresses.append(int(item, 16))
    addresses = sorted(set(addresses))
    eus = {p.stem: Elf(p) for p in sorted((ROOT / args.eu / "elf").glob("*.elf"))}
    uss = {p.stem: Elf(p) for p in sorted((ROOT / args.us / "elf").glob("*.elf"))}
    indexes: dict = {}
    for address in addresses:
        results = []
        for name, eu in eus.items():
            if not eu.holds(address) or name not in uss:
                continue
            us = uss[name]
            idx = indexes.setdefault(name, index(us))
            how, mapped = "code", map_code(eu, us, address, idx) if looks_like_code(eu, address) else None
            if mapped is None:
                how, mapped = "data", map_data(eu, us, address, idx)
            if mapped is not None:
                results.append(f"{mapped:08x} ({name}, {how})")
        if not results:
            # Data no code of its own ELF builds (a main class's vtable set up by an overlay): any ELF's code.
            for name, eu in eus.items():
                if name not in uss:
                    continue
                mapped = map_data(eu, uss[name], address, indexes.setdefault(name, index(uss[name])))
                if mapped is not None:
                    results.append(f"{mapped:08x} (from {name} code, data)")
                    break
        print(f"{address:08x} -> " + ("; ".join(dict.fromkeys(results)) if results else "?"))
        sys.stdout.flush()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/slz.py`:

```py
"""tri-Ace SLZ / SLE decompressor (Valkyrie Profile 2, SLES-54645).

Reimplemented from the game's own routines:
  FUN_001025d0  header check, SLE decryption, type dispatch, chunk chaining
  FUN_00101ea0  type 1: byte LZSS, ends on distance 0
  FUN_001018d0  type 2: byte LZSS + RLE, ends at decompressed size
  FUN_00101370  type 3: 16-bit word LZSS, ends on distance 0
  type 0: stored

Chunk header (16 bytes, little endian):
  0x0 magic 'SLZ' (or 'SLE' = encrypted payload)   0x3 type (u8)
  0x4 compressed size   0x8 decompressed size   0xC offset to next chunk (0 = last)
"""
import os
import pathlib
import struct

HEADER = struct.Struct("<3sBIII")

KEY_FILE = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "build" / "sle_key.txt"


def load_sle_key() -> bytes | None:
    """The 16-byte SLE key is not distributed: VP2S_SLE_KEY or build/sle_key.txt (32 hex digits).

    The game computes it at boot on VU0 and keeps it at 0x1d5930 (docs/RECOMPILER.md); it can
    also be recovered by frequency analysis of the two SLE chunks (LBA 255055, 900723), checked
    by exact-size decompression of both.
    """
    text = os.environ.get("VP2S_SLE_KEY") or (KEY_FILE.read_text() if KEY_FILE.is_file() else "")
    text = text.strip()
    return bytes.fromhex(text) if text else None


SLE_KEY = load_sle_key()


class SlzError(Exception):
    pass


def decrypt_sle(payload: bytes, key: bytes) -> bytes:
    """SLE payload: b = (b - k) ^ key[i % 16], k starts at 3 and grows by 3 per byte."""
    out = bytearray(payload)
    k = 3
    for i in range(len(out)):
        out[i] = ((out[i] - k) & 0xFF) ^ key[i & 0xF]
        k = (k + 3) & 0xFF
    return bytes(out)


def _type1(src: bytes, limit: int | None = None) -> bytes:
    out = bytearray()
    i = 0
    bits = 0
    left = 0
    while limit is None or len(out) < limit:
        if left == 0:
            bits = src[i]
            i += 1
            left = 8
        flag = bits & 1
        bits >>= 1
        left -= 1
        if flag:
            out.append(src[i])
            i += 1
            continue
        dist = src[i] | (src[i + 1] & 0xF) << 8
        if dist == 0:
            return bytes(out)
        n = (src[i + 1] >> 4) + 3
        i += 2
        for _ in range(n):
            out.append(out[-dist])
    return bytes(out)


def _type2(src: bytes, size: int) -> bytes:
    out = bytearray()
    i = 0
    bits = 0
    left = 0
    while len(out) < size:
        if left == 0:
            bits = src[i]
            i += 1
            left = 8
        flag = bits & 1
        bits >>= 1
        left -= 1
        if flag:
            out.append(src[i])
            i += 1
            continue
        b0, b1 = src[i], src[i + 1]
        dist = b0 | (b1 & 0xF) << 8
        n = b1 >> 4
        if n < 0xF:
            for _ in range(n + 3):
                out.append(out[-dist])
        else:  # RLE
            if dist < 0x100:
                value = src[i + 2]
                count = dist + 0x13
                i += 1
            else:
                value = b0
                count = (dist >> 8) + 3
            out.extend(bytes([value]) * count)
        i += 2
    return bytes(out[:size])


def _type3(src: bytes, limit: int | None = None) -> bytes:
    words = struct.unpack_from(f"<{len(src) // 2}H", src)
    out = []
    i = 0
    bits = 0
    left = 0
    while limit is None or len(out) * 2 < limit:
        if left == 0:
            bits = words[i]
            i += 1
            left = 16
        flag = bits & 1
        bits >>= 1
        left -= 1
        if flag:
            out.append(words[i])
            i += 1
            continue
        w = words[i]
        dist = w & 0xFFF
        if dist == 0:
            return struct.pack(f"<{len(out)}H", *out)
        for _ in range((w >> 12) + 2):
            out.append(out[-dist])
        i += 1
    return struct.pack(f"<{len(out)}H", *out)


def parse_header(buf: bytes, off: int = 0):
    magic, typ, comp, decomp, nxt = HEADER.unpack_from(buf, off)
    if magic not in (b"SLZ", b"SLE"):
        raise SlzError(f"bad magic {magic!r} at 0x{off:x}")
    return magic, typ, comp, decomp, nxt


def decompress_chunk(buf: bytes, off: int = 0, key: bytes | None = None, peek: int | None = None) -> bytes:
    """Decompress one chunk; with peek, stop after about that many output bytes (no size check)."""
    magic, typ, comp, decomp, _ = parse_header(buf, off)
    payload = buf[off + 16:off + 16 + comp]
    if magic == b"SLE":
        if key is None:
            raise SlzError("SLE chunk needs the 16-byte key: set VP2S_SLE_KEY or write build/sle_key.txt")
        payload = decrypt_sle(payload, key)
    if typ == 0:
        data = payload[:decomp]
    elif typ == 1:
        data = _type1(payload, peek)
    elif typ == 2:
        data = _type2(payload, min(decomp, peek) if peek else decomp)
    else:
        data = _type3(payload, peek)
    if peek:
        return data[:peek]
    if len(data) != decomp:
        raise SlzError(f"type {typ}: got {len(data)} bytes, header says {decomp}")
    return data


def read_chain(f, off: int) -> bytes:
    """Read a whole chunk chain from a file, following each header's 'next' field."""
    f.seek(off)
    buf = bytearray(f.read(16))
    pos = 0
    while True:
        _, _, comp, _, nxt = parse_header(buf, pos)
        end = pos + 16 + comp
        if nxt:
            end = max(end, pos + nxt + 16)
        if len(buf) < end:
            buf += f.read(end - len(buf))
        if not nxt:
            return bytes(buf[:pos + 16 + comp])
        pos += nxt


def decompress_chain(buf: bytes, off: int = 0, key: bytes | None = None) -> list[bytes]:
    """Decompress every chunk of a chain linked by the 'next' field."""
    parts = []
    while True:
        parts.append(decompress_chunk(buf, off, key))
        nxt = parse_header(buf, off)[4]
        if nxt == 0:
            return parts
        off += nxt

```

`tools/vp2_adc_probe.py`:

```py
"""Probe of VP2's mesh microprogram XYZF2 w word (fog and ADC) against recorded runs.

    python tools/vp2_adc_probe.py <trace.vu1rec> [runs]

Hypotheses: fog = clamp(mem[68].z + mem[68].w * Q, 0, 255) (lit path, mem[15].w != 0), ADC set for a strip's first
two vertices, for a vertex whose last three vertices include one outside the clip volume of the matrix at mem[85..88]
(|x|, |y| or |z| > |w|), and for a back-facing triangle (sign of the screen cross product times mem[16].z, the sign
alternating along the strip).
"""
import sys
import collections

sys.path.insert(0, __file__.rsplit('\\', 1)[0].rsplit('/', 1)[0])
import vp2_mesh_proto as m


def main():
    path = sys.argv[1]
    limit = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 3000
    stats = collections.Counter()
    code = None
    runs = 0
    for run in m.read_records(path):
        if run['code'] is not None:
            code = run['code']
        if run['start'] != 0x0880 or run['resume']:
            continue
        runs += 1
        if runs > limit:
            break
        memory = bytearray(run['data_before'])
        if m.run_setup(run, memory) or not m.run_lighting(run, memory, run['state_before'], code):
            continue
        state = run['state_before']
        top = run['top']
        h0 = m.qword_i(memory, top)
        sign0 = m.qword_f(memory, 16)[2]
        fogp = m.qword_f(memory, 68)
        lit = m.qword_i(memory, 15)[3] != 0
        mat = [m.vf(state, 1), m.vf(state, 2), m.vf(state, 3), m.vf(state, 4)]
        clip = m.matrix(memory, 85)
        want = m.packet_vertices(run['packets'])
        v = top + h0[1]
        k_all = 0
        for s in range(h0[2]):
            count = m.qword_i(memory, top + h0[3] + s)[0] & 0xFFFF
            screens = []
            outside = []
            sign = sign0
            for k in range(count):
                p = m.qword_f(memory, v + 3)
                c = m.transform(mat, p, 1.0)
                q = 1.0 / c[3] if c[3] else 0.0
                screens.append((c[0] * q, c[1] * q))
                cc = m.transform(clip, p, 1.0)
                outside.append(any(abs(cc[i]) > abs(cc[3]) for i in range(3)))
                if k_all < len(want):
                    w = want[k_all]
                    xyzw = w['xyz']
                    adc = xyzw[3]
                    if lit:
                        fog = int(max(0.0, min(fogp[2] + fogp[3] * q, 255.0)))
                        stats['fog ok' if True else ''] += 0
                    clipped = any(outside[max(0, k - 2):k + 1])
                    back = None
                    if k >= 2:
                        a, b, cpt = screens[k - 2], screens[k - 1], screens[k]
                        e1 = (a[0] - b[0], a[1] - b[1])
                        e2 = (b[0] - cpt[0], b[1] - cpt[1])
                        cross = e1[0] * e2[1] - e1[1] * e2[0]
                        back = cross * sign
                    predicted = k < 2 or clipped
                    cat = ('k%d' % k if k < 2 else 'clip' if clipped else
                           ('back>0 s16=%g' % sign0) if back and back > 0 else 'back<=0')
                    stats[(cat, 'adc' if adc else 'draw')] += 1
                sign = -sign
                k_all += 1
                v += 4
    for key, value in sorted(stats.items(), key=lambda kv: str(kv[0])):
        print(key, value)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

```

`tools/vp2_archives.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""VP2's encrypted archives ('p@Ck', the battle data among them) unpacked offline, beside tools/extract.py's assets.

329 files of the game's archive are packs whose member table and members are encrypted with a per-file key k
(mod027 FUN_00366A00 / FUN_00365F70): extract.py's scan for SLZ chains cannot see inside them, so the battle's models and
textures had no asset. This finds each pack's k from its own table, decrypts the members the game decrypts, and writes
every SLZ part found in them as build/extract/assets/<nibble>/<chain disc offset:010x>_<part:03d>.<ext>, the name the
port computes at run time from the game's own reads (port/vp2s_loads.cpp). Game data: build tree only, never committed.

  tools/vp2_archives.py <game.iso> [--out build/extract/assets]

Formats (docs/vp2_models.md):
  TOC: 0x9000 bytes at disc 0x200000 (sector 0x400), three tables of 0xC00 u32 (start sector, sectors, other), XOR'd
       by a stream seeded 0x49287491 (FUN_0011EBC0).
  Pack: entry table at +8 of {u32 offset | 0x80000000 encrypted, u32 id}, ending {end, 0}, each word XOR'd by
       a = a * 2053 + 0x3619 from a = (k ^ 0x5E) * 29. Encrypted members, in order, through one word stream:
       A = (k ^ 0xF3) * 0x2AB7D + 0x33E, V = (k ^ 0xC7) * 3, P = A * 129 + 0x1CBC; per word sh = (V >> 16) & 31,
       out = (w ^ P ^ (A << sh)) - (V >> 1), A += 0x718331, V = V * 1056777 + 0x40001, P = out.
"""
import argparse
import os
import re
import struct
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import slz  # noqa: E402

M = 0xFFFFFFFF
SECTOR = 2048


def s32(x):
    x &= M
    return x - (1 << 32) if x & 0x80000000 else x


def read_toc(iso):
    iso.seek(0x200000)
    raw = iso.read(0x9000)
    t = list(struct.unpack('<%dI' % (0x9000 // 4), raw))
    seed = 0x49287491
    x = seed
    for i in range(0xC00):
        t[i] ^= x
        x = (x ^ (x << 1)) & M
        t[0xC00 + i] ^= x
        y = (~x ^ seed) & M
        t[0x1800 + i] ^= y
        x = (y ^ (y << 2) ^ seed) & M
    t[0] = 0x400
    return [(t[i] & 0xFFFFFF, t[0xC00 + i] & 0xFFFFFF) for i in range(0xC00)]


def terminated(d, a0, size):
    """The number of table words up to and with the terminator {end, 0} (end within 2 sectors of size), its other
    entries' offsets increasing, or None."""
    a, w = a0, []
    for i in range(min(512, (len(d) - 8) // 4)):
        a = (a * 2053 + 0x3619) & M
        w.append(struct.unpack_from('<I', d, 8 + 4 * i)[0] ^ a)
        if i & 1 and i >= 3 and w[i] == 0 and abs((w[i - 1] & 0x7FFFFFFF) - size) <= 2 * SECTOR:
            offsets = [w[j] & 0x7FFFFFFF for j in range(2, i, 2)]
            if all(b > c for c, b in zip(offsets, offsets[1:])) and offsets and offsets[0] >= 8 + 4 * (i + 1):
                return i + 1
            return None
    return None


def entry_table(d, k, size=None):
    a0 = ((k ^ 0x5E) * 29) & M
    x = (a0 * 2053 + 0x3619) & M
    if len(d) < 16:
        return None
    first = (struct.unpack_from('<I', d, 8)[0] ^ x) & 0x7FFFFFFF
    n = first // 4 - 2
    if not (2 <= n < 4096) or n & 1 or 8 + 4 * n > len(d):
        # Some packs' first word does not decode (the battle's file 1781: 0x73F33D7D where 0x80000038 belongs, the
        # other entries fine): the table found by its terminator {end of the file, 0} instead.
        n = terminated(d, a0, size or len(d))
        if n is None:
            return None
        first = 0x80000000 | (8 + 4 * n)  # encrypted: the stream must go through it for the next members to decrypt
    w = list(struct.unpack_from('<%dI' % n, d, 8))
    a = a0
    for i in range(n):
        a = (a * 2053 + 0x3619) & M
        w[i] ^= a
    if (w[0] & 0x7FFFFFFF) != 8 + 4 * n:
        w[0] = first
    entries = [(w[2 * i], w[2 * i + 1]) for i in range(n // 2)]
    while len(entries) > 2 and entries[-1] == (0, 0):  # the table padded to its size after the terminator
        entries.pop()
    offsets = [e[0] & 0x7FFFFFFF for e in entries]
    if offsets[0] != 8 + 4 * n or offsets[0] < 0x18 or any(b < a for a, b in zip(offsets, offsets[1:])):
        return None
    if entries[-1][1] != 0 or abs(offsets[-1] - (size or len(d))) > 2 * SECTOR:
        return None
    return entries


def nested(member):
    """A nested pack's (key, entries): an encrypted table (its key found as for a file), or 'p@Ck' with key byte 0 and
    the table in the clear (+6 the entry count), or None."""
    inner = next((k for k in range(256) if entry_table(member, k, len(member))), None)
    if inner is not None:
        return inner, entry_table(member, inner, len(member))
    if member[:4] == b'p@Ck' and member[5] == 0:
        count, = struct.unpack_from('<H', member, 6)
        if 1 <= count < 4096 and 8 + 8 * (count + 1) <= len(member):
            entries = [struct.unpack_from('<II', member, 8 + 8 * i) for i in range(count + 1)]
            offsets = [e[0] & 0x7FFFFFFF for e in entries]
            if entries[-1][1] == 0 and offsets[-1] <= len(member) and all(b >= a for a, b in zip(offsets, offsets[1:])):
                return 0, entries
    return None


class Stream:
    def __init__(self, k):
        self.A = ((k ^ 0xF3) * 0x2AB7D + 0x33E) & M
        self.V = ((k ^ 0xC7) * 3) & M
        self.P = (self.A * 129 + 0x1CBC) & M

    def decrypt(self, data):
        n = len(data) // 4
        w = struct.unpack('<%dI' % n, data[:4 * n])
        out = []
        A, V, P = self.A, self.V, self.P
        for x in w:
            sh = (s32(V) >> 16) & 0x1F
            t4 = (A << sh) & M
            A = (A + 0x718331) & M
            o = ((x ^ P ^ t4) - (s32(V) >> 1)) & M
            V = (V * 1056777 + 0x40001) & M
            out.append(o)
            P = o
        self.A, self.V, self.P = A, V, P
        return struct.pack('<%dI' % n, *out) + data[4 * n:]


CHAIN = re.compile(rb'S(LZ|LE)[\x00-\x03]')


def chains(data):
    """Every SLZ chain in data: (offset, [decompressed parts])."""
    pos = 0
    while True:
        m = CHAIN.search(data, pos)
        if not m:
            return
        p = m.start()
        _, typ, comp, dsz, nxt = struct.unpack_from('<3sB3I', data, p)
        if not (0 < comp < 16 << 20 and 0 < dsz < 64 << 20):
            pos = p + 1
            continue
        parts, q = [], p
        try:
            while True:
                parts.append(slz.decompress_chunk(data, q, slz.SLE_KEY))
                n = struct.unpack_from('<I', data, q + 12)[0]
                if not n:
                    break
                q += n
        except Exception:
            pos = p + 1
            continue
        yield p, parts
        pos = q + 16


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    root = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
    ap.add_argument('iso')
    ap.add_argument('--out', default=os.path.join(root, 'build', 'extract', 'assets'))
    a = ap.parse_args()
    if slz.SLE_KEY is None:
        print('no SLE key (VP2S_SLE_KEY or build/sle_key.txt)')
        return 1
    iso = open(a.iso, 'rb')
    toc = read_toc(iso)
    packs = written = 0
    for fid, (lba, sectors) in enumerate(toc):
        if fid == 0 or sectors == 0 or sectors > 0x8000:
            continue
        iso.seek(lba * SECTOR)
        head = iso.read(min(sectors * SECTOR, 0x10000))
        key = next((k for k in range(256) if entry_table(head, k, sectors * SECTOR)), None)
        if key is None:
            continue
        iso.seek(lba * SECTOR)
        d = iso.read(sectors * SECTOR)
        entries = entry_table(d, key)
        if not entries:
            continue
        packs += 1
        stream = Stream(key)
        for i, (offset, ident) in enumerate(entries[:-1]):
            start, end = offset & 0x7FFFFFFF, entries[i + 1][0] & 0x7FFFFFFF
            member = d[start:end]
            pieces = []  # (offset in the file, bytes) to scan for chains
            if offset & 0x80000000:
                member = stream.decrypt(member)
            sub = nested(member) if (ident & 0xFF00) == 0x5000 else None
            if sub:
                # A member of type 0x5000 is a nested pack (mod028 FUN_003B6E30's slots 3..6): its own key, a fresh
                # stream, its members holding the chains (the battle characters; the battle interface's sheets in
                # file 1781, whose nested pack is itself an encrypted member with a plain table).
                inner, sub = sub
                s2 = Stream(inner)
                for j, (soffset, _) in enumerate(sub[:-1]):
                    sstart, send = soffset & 0x7FFFFFFF, sub[j + 1][0] & 0x7FFFFFFF
                    piece = member[sstart:send]
                    if soffset & 0x80000000:
                        piece = s2.decrypt(piece)
                    pieces.append((start + sstart, piece))
            else:
                pieces.append((start, member))
            for base, piece in pieces:
              found = False
              for at, parts in chains(piece):
                found = True
                disc = lba * SECTOR + base + at
                folder = os.path.join(a.out, '%x' % (disc >> 28))
                os.makedirs(folder, exist_ok=True)
                for n, part in enumerate(parts):
                    ext = 'pack' if part[:4] == b'p@Ck' else 'bin'
                    path = os.path.join(folder, '%010x_%03d.%s' % (disc, n, ext))
                    if not os.path.exists(path):
                        with open(path, 'wb') as f:
                            f.write(part)
                        written += 1
              if not found and len(piece) >= 0x40:
                  # A member stored without compression (the battle interface's textures among them): kept as it is,
                  # <disc offset of the member:010x>_raw.bin, for the texture and mesh conversions to find.
                  disc = lba * SECTOR + base
                  folder = os.path.join(a.out, '%x' % (disc >> 28))
                  os.makedirs(folder, exist_ok=True)
                  path = os.path.join(folder, '%010x_raw.bin' % disc)
                  if not os.path.exists(path):
                      with open(path, 'wb') as f:
                          f.write(piece)
                      written += 1
    print(f'packs: {packs}, parts written: {written}')
    return 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())

```

`tools/vp2_mesh_proto.py`:

```py
"""Prototype of VP2's native mesh decode, checked against recorded VU1 runs.

    python tools/vp2_mesh_proto.py <trace.vu1rec> [max_runs]

For each run of the mesh microprogram (start pc 0x0880, see docs/vp2_vu1.md) the object is decoded from VU1 data
memory without running the microcode: header at TOP, vertices (4 qwords, position at +3), triangle strips, and the
4x4 matrix in vf01..vf04. The screen positions it computes are compared with the XYZ the microprogram sent to the GS
(the run's PATH1 packets). First pass: positions are taken after the run's set-up routines (data memory after the
run), so skinning is not reimplemented yet.
"""
import struct
import sys

MAGIC = 0x52315556
MEMORY = 16 * 1024


def read_records(path):
    data = open(path, 'rb').read()
    pos = 0
    state_size = None
    while pos + 28 <= len(data):
        magic, resume, start, top, itop, max_cycles, has_code = struct.unpack_from('<7I', data, pos)
        if magic != MAGIC:
            raise ValueError('bad record at %d' % pos)
        pos += 28
        code = None
        if has_code:
            code = data[pos:pos + MEMORY]
            pos += MEMORY
        if state_size is None:
            # VU1State's size depends on the compiler's padding: find the one that lands on the packet count and
            # the next record's magic.
            for size in range(640, 720, 4):
                count_at = pos + size + MEMORY + size + MEMORY
                if count_at + 4 > len(data):
                    continue
                count = struct.unpack_from('<I', data, count_at)[0]
                p = count_at + 4
                ok = count < 4096
                for _ in range(count if ok else 0):
                    if p + 4 > len(data):
                        ok = False
                        break
                    p += 4 + struct.unpack_from('<I', data, p)[0]
                if ok and (p == len(data) or (p + 4 <= len(data) and struct.unpack_from('<I', data, p)[0] == MAGIC)):
                    state_size = size
                    break
            if state_size is None:
                raise ValueError('cannot find the VU1State size')
        state_before = data[pos:pos + state_size]
        pos += state_size
        data_before = data[pos:pos + MEMORY]
        pos += MEMORY
        state_after = data[pos:pos + state_size]
        pos += state_size
        data_after = data[pos:pos + MEMORY]
        pos += MEMORY
        count = struct.unpack_from('<I', data, pos)[0]
        pos += 4
        packets = []
        for _ in range(count):
            size = struct.unpack_from('<I', data, pos)[0]
            packets.append(data[pos + 4:pos + 4 + size])
            pos += 4 + size
        yield dict(resume=resume, start=start, top=top, itop=itop, code=code, state_before=state_before,
                   data_before=data_before, state_after=state_after, data_after=data_after, packets=packets)


def vf(state, index):
    return struct.unpack_from('<4f', state, index * 16)


def qword_i(memory, qw):
    return struct.unpack_from('<4i', memory, (qw & 0x3FF) * 16)


def qword_f(memory, qw):
    return struct.unpack_from('<4f', memory, (qw & 0x3FF) * 16)


def packet_xyz(packets):
    """XYZ2/XYZ3 of PACKED GIF packets, in order: (x, y, z, adc) with x, y in 1/16 pixel."""
    out = []
    for packet in packets:
        p = 0
        while p + 16 <= len(packet):
            lo, hi = struct.unpack_from('<QQ', packet, p)
            p += 16
            nloop = lo & 0x7FFF
            flg = (lo >> 58) & 3
            nreg = (lo >> 60) & 0xF or 16
            if flg != 0:
                break
            regs = [(hi >> (4 * i)) & 0xF for i in range(nreg)]
            for _ in range(nloop):
                for reg in regs:
                    if p + 16 > len(packet):
                        return out
                    w = struct.unpack_from('<4I', packet, p)
                    p += 16
                    if reg in (0x4, 0x5):  # XYZF2 / XYZ2 packed: x, y 16 bits, z 32 bits, ADC bit 15 of w's top
                        out.append((w[0] & 0xFFFF, w[1] & 0xFFFF, w[2], (w[3] >> 15) & 1))
            if (lo >> 15) & 1:
                break
    return out


def set_f(memory, qw, values, mask='xyzw'):
    for i, c in enumerate('xyzw'):
        if c in mask:
            struct.pack_into('<f', memory, (qw & 0x3FF) * 16 + i * 4, values[i])


def matrix(memory, qw):
    return [qword_f(memory, qw + r) for r in range(4)]


def transform(m, v, w):
    return [m[0][i] * v[0] + m[1][i] * v[1] + m[2][i] * v[2] + m[3][i] * w for i in range(4)]


def run_setup(run, memory):
    """The object's set-up routines (header 5.x..w, data at 4.x..w), done natively on a copy of VU memory: 8 integer
    normals (12-bit) and positions (12.4) to float, 2 rigid skinning, 4 two-bone skinning, 6 integer normals only.
    Returns the routines it did not know."""
    top = run['top']
    h2, h4, h5 = qword_i(memory, top + 2), qword_i(memory, top + 4), qword_i(memory, top + 5)
    vertices = top + qword_i(memory, top)[1]
    count = h2[1]
    unknown = []
    for routine, data in zip(h5, h4):
        if routine in (0, 46):
            continue
        base = top + data
        if routine == 8:
            for k in range(count):
                v = vertices + 4 * k
                n = qword_i(memory, v)
                p = qword_i(memory, v + 3)
                set_f(memory, v, [n[0] / 4096.0, n[1] / 4096.0, n[2] / 4096.0, 0.0], 'xyz')
                set_f(memory, v + 3, [p[0] / 16.0, p[1] / 16.0, p[2] / 16.0, 0.0], 'xyz')
        elif routine == 6:
            for k in range(count):
                v = vertices + 4 * k
                n = qword_i(memory, v)
                set_f(memory, v, [n[0] / 4096.0, n[1] / 4096.0, n[2] / 4096.0, 0.0], 'xyz')
        elif routine == 2:
            for k in range(count + 1):
                v = vertices + 4 * k
                bone = qword_i(memory, v + 3)[3]
                m = matrix(memory, bone)
                n = qword_f(memory, v)
                p = qword_f(memory, v + 3)
                set_f(memory, v, transform(m, n, 0.0), 'xyz')
                set_f(memory, v + 3, transform(m, p, 1.0), 'xyzw')
        elif routine == 4:
            for k in range(count):
                v = vertices + 4 * k
                e = qword_i(memory, base + k)
                ef = qword_f(memory, base + k)
                a, b = matrix(memory, e[0]), matrix(memory, e[2])
                m = [[a[r][c] * ef[1] + b[r][c] * ef[3] for c in range(4)] for r in range(4)]
                n = qword_f(memory, v)
                p = qword_f(memory, v + 3)
                set_f(memory, v, transform(m, n, 0.0), 'xyz')
                set_f(memory, v + 3, transform(m, p, 1.0), 'xyz')
        elif routine == 48:
            # Texture scrolling: an offset (S, T in .zw) picked by the routine's data, added to every vertex's UV.
            index = qword_i(memory, base)[0]
            off = qword_f(memory, 38 + index)
            for k in range(count):
                v = vertices + 4 * k
                uv = list(qword_i(memory, v + 1))
                for c in range(2):
                    uv[c] = int((uv[c] / 4096.0 + off[2 + c]) * 4096.0)
                struct.pack_into('<2i', memory, (v + 1 & 0x3FF) * 16, uv[0], uv[1])
        else:
            unknown.append(routine)
    return unknown


def library_layout(code):
    """Where this build of the microcode library keeps its tables, found by following its code from 0x0880: the
    BAL at 0x08a8 to the set-up dispatcher, whose IADDIU vi09 gives the set-up table (instruction index) and whose
    IADDIU vi01, vi01, n give the lighting tables A, B, C, D in that order (D the last). Returns (setup, D) or None."""
    if code is None:
        return None
    lo = struct.unpack_from('<I', code, 0x08a8)[0]
    if (lo >> 25) & 0x7F != 0x21:
        return None
    imm = lo & 0x7FF
    imm = imm - 0x800 if imm & 0x400 else imm
    sub = 0x08a8 + 8 + imm * 8
    setup = None
    tables = []
    for pc in range(sub, min(sub + 0x400, len(code)), 8):
        lo = struct.unpack_from('<I', code, pc)[0]
        if (lo >> 25) & 0x7F == 0x08:  # IADDIU
            it, is_ = (lo >> 16) & 0x1F, (lo >> 11) & 0x1F
            value = (((lo >> 21) & 0xF) << 11) | (lo & 0x7FF)
            if it == 9 and is_ == 0 and setup is None:
                setup = value
            elif it == 1 and is_ == 1:
                tables.append(value)
    if setup is None or not tables:
        return None
    return setup, max(tables)


def light_w_clamped(code):
    """Whether lighting mode 1 clamps the fourth light's term at 0 (maxx.xyzw) or lets it go negative (maxx.xyz, a
    darkening light): the code image's own choice, read from the first MAX(bc x) after the routine's entry. Table D
    entry 1 is at 0x2b20 in the builds seen so far."""
    if code is None:
        return True
    target = None
    layout = library_layout(code)
    entry = (layout[1] + 2) * 8 if layout else 0x2b20
    lo = struct.unpack_from('<I', code, entry)[0]
    if (lo >> 25) & 0x7F == 0x20:
        imm = lo & 0x7FF
        imm = imm - 0x800 if imm & 0x400 else imm
        target = entry + 8 + imm * 8
    if target is None:
        return True
    for pc in range(target, target + 0x100, 8):
        up = struct.unpack_from('<I', code, pc + 4)[0]
        if (up & 0x3F) == 0x10:  # MAXx
            return ((up >> 21) & 1) != 0
    return True


def run_lighting(run, memory, state, code=None):
    """Vertex colours (+2) lit natively, for the lighting the field uses (mem[14].w = 0, table D): mode 0 colour times
    mem[13], mode 1 four directional lights (directions vf05..vf07 by component, colours mem[5..8],
    ambient mem[19], alpha mem[13].w) times the colour. Clamped to [0, mem[0]], truncated like ftoi0. Returns False for
    a mode not done yet."""
    top = run['top']
    mode = qword_i(memory, top + 8)[1]
    flags = qword_i(memory, 14)
    count = qword_i(memory, top + 2)[1]
    vertices = top + qword_i(memory, top)[1]
    limit = qword_f(memory, 0)
    if flags[3] != 0:
        return False

    def apply(v, factor):
        c = qword_i(memory, v + 2)
        out = [int(max(0.0, min(c[i] * factor[i], limit[i]))) for i in range(4)]
        struct.pack_into('<4i', memory, (v + 2 & 0x3FF) * 16, *out)

    if mode == 0:
        # Seen on the traces: with mem[14].x the colour is scaled by mem[13]; without it RGB goes to 0 and only the
        # alpha is scaled (the entry branches from a branch's delay slot; to confirm against the code).
        scale = list(qword_f(memory, 13))
        if not flags[0]:
            scale[0] = scale[1] = scale[2] = 0.0
        for k in range(count):
            apply(vertices + 4 * k, scale)
        return True
    if mode == 1:
        d = [vf(state, 5), vf(state, 6), vf(state, 7)]
        colours = [qword_f(memory, 5 + i) for i in range(4)]
        ambient = qword_f(memory, 19)
        alpha = qword_f(memory, 13)[3]
        clamp_w = light_w_clamped(code)
        for k in range(count):
            v = vertices + 4 * k
            n = qword_f(memory, v)
            dots = [d[0][i] * n[0] + d[1][i] * n[1] + d[2][i] * n[2] for i in range(4)]
            dots = [max(0.0, x) if (i < 3 or clamp_w) else x for i, x in enumerate(dots)]
            factor = [sum(colours[i][c] * dots[i] for i in range(4)) + ambient[c] for c in range(3)] + [alpha]
            apply(v, factor)
        return True
    return False


def packet_vertices(packets):
    """Per vertex of PACKED GIF packets: dict of st (S, T, Q floats), rgba (ints), xyz (x, y, z, adc)."""
    out = []
    current = {}
    for packet in packets:
        p = 0
        while p + 16 <= len(packet):
            lo, hi = struct.unpack_from('<QQ', packet, p)
            p += 16
            nloop = lo & 0x7FFF
            flg = (lo >> 58) & 3
            nreg = (lo >> 60) & 0xF or 16
            if flg != 0:
                break
            regs = [(hi >> (4 * i)) & 0xF for i in range(nreg)]
            for _ in range(nloop):
                for reg in regs:
                    if p + 16 > len(packet):
                        return out
                    if reg == 0x2:
                        current['st'] = struct.unpack_from('<3f', packet, p)
                    elif reg == 0x1:
                        current['rgba'] = struct.unpack_from('<4I', packet, p)
                    elif reg in (0x4, 0x5):
                        w = struct.unpack_from('<4I', packet, p)
                        current['xyz'] = (w[0] & 0xFFFF, w[1] & 0xFFFF, w[2], (w[3] >> 15) & 1)
                        current['fog'] = (w[3] >> 4) & 0xFF if reg == 0x4 else None
                        current['raw'] = w
                        out.append(dict(current))
                    p += 16
            if (lo >> 15) & 1:
                break
    return out


def decode_full(run, data, state):
    """Each vertex: screen x, y, z (float, before ftoi4), S, T, Q, RGBA."""
    top = run['top']
    h0 = qword_i(data, top)
    vertices_at = top + h0[1]
    strips_at = top + h0[3]
    m = [vf(state, 1), vf(state, 2), vf(state, 3), vf(state, 4)]
    out = []
    v = vertices_at
    for s in range(h0[2]):
        for _ in range(qword_i(data, strips_at + s)[0] & 0xFFFF):
            p = qword_f(data, v + 3)
            c = transform(m, p, 1.0)
            q = 1.0 / c[3] if c[3] != 0.0 else 0.0
            uv = qword_i(data, v + 1)
            out.append(dict(xyz=(c[0] * q, c[1] * q, c[2] * q), st=(uv[0] / 4096.0 * q, uv[1] / 4096.0 * q, uv[2] / 4096.0 * q),
                            rgba=qword_i(data, v + 2)))
            v += 4
    return out


def decode_packets(run, data, state):
    """Each vertex as the GS gets it: S, T, Q, RGBA, XYZF2 (x, y, z as ftoi4 gives them, fog, adc), and the strip it
    starts (True on a strip's first vertex). ADC: a strip's first two vertices; any of the last three vertices
    outside the clip volume of the matrix at mem[85..88]; a back-facing triangle when the object culls (header 15.x
    + mem[41].z == 0: screen cross product of the last three, sign alternating along the strip, positive = back).
    Fog (mem[15].w != 0): mem[68].z + mem[68].w * Q, clamped to 0..255."""
    top = run['top']
    h0 = qword_i(data, top)
    m = [vf(state, 1), vf(state, 2), vf(state, 3), vf(state, 4)]
    clip = matrix(data, 85)
    culls = qword_i(data, top + 15)[0] + qword_i(data, 41)[2] == 0
    lit = qword_i(data, 15)[3] != 0
    fogp = qword_f(data, 68)
    out = []
    v = top + h0[1]
    for s in range(h0[2]):
        screens = []
        outside = []
        for k in range(qword_i(data, top + h0[3] + s)[0] & 0xFFFF):
            p = qword_f(data, v + 3)
            c = transform(m, p, 1.0)
            q = 1.0 / c[3] if c[3] != 0.0 else 0.0
            x, y, z = c[0] * q, c[1] * q, c[2] * q
            screens.append((x, y))
            cc = transform(clip, p, 1.0)
            outside.append(any(abs(cc[i]) > abs(cc[3]) for i in range(3)))
            adc = k < 2 or any(outside[max(0, k - 2):k + 1])
            if not adc and culls:
                a, b, d = screens[k - 2], screens[k - 1], screens[k]
                cross = (a[0] - b[0]) * (b[1] - d[1]) - (a[1] - b[1]) * (b[0] - d[0])
                adc = cross * (1 if k % 2 == 0 else -1) > 0
            fog = int(max(0.0, min(fogp[2] + fogp[3] * q, 255.0))) if lit else None
            uv = qword_i(data, v + 1)
            out.append(dict(st=(uv[0] / 4096.0 * q, uv[1] / 4096.0 * q, uv[2] / 4096.0 * q), rgba=qword_i(data, v + 2),
                            xyz=(int(x * 16), int(y * 16), int(z * 16)), fog=fog, adc=adc, first=k == 0, k=k))
            v += 4
    return out


def decode(run, data, state):
    top = run['top']
    h0 = qword_i(data, top)
    vertices_at = top + h0[1]
    strips_at = top + h0[3]
    strip_count = h0[2]
    m = [vf(state, 1), vf(state, 2), vf(state, 3), vf(state, 4)]
    out = []
    v = vertices_at
    for s in range(strip_count):
        count = qword_i(data, strips_at + s)[0]
        for _ in range(count & 0xFFFF):
            px, py, pz, _ = qword_f(data, v + 3)
            x = m[0][0] * px + m[1][0] * py + m[2][0] * pz + m[3][0]
            y = m[0][1] * px + m[1][1] * py + m[2][1] * pz + m[3][1]
            z = m[0][2] * px + m[1][2] * py + m[2][2] * pz + m[3][2]
            w = m[0][3] * px + m[1][3] * py + m[2][3] * pz + m[3][3]
            if w != 0.0:
                out.append((x / w, y / w, z / w))
            else:
                out.append((0.0, 0.0, 0.0))
            v += 4
    return out


def main():
    path = sys.argv[1]
    limit = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 and sys.argv[2].isdigit() else 20
    native = '--native' in sys.argv
    checked = matched = skipped = 0
    code = None
    for run in read_records(path):
        if run['code'] is not None:
            code = run['code']
        if run['start'] != 0x0880 or run['resume'] != 0:
            continue
        if native:
            memory = bytearray(run['data_before'])
            unknown = run_setup(run, memory)
            if unknown:
                skipped += 1
                continue
            got = decode(run, memory, run['state_before'])
        else:
            got = decode(run, run['data_after'], run['state_after'])
        want = packet_xyz(run['packets'])
        n = min(len(got), len(want))
        # ftoi4: x, y, z times 16 as integers; x and y land in 16 bits (wrapping), z in 32.
        fixed = lambda value, bits: int(value * 16) & ((1 << bits) - 1)
        close = sum(1 for g, w in zip(got, want)
                    if abs(fixed(g[0], 16) - w[0]) <= 1 and abs(fixed(g[1], 16) - w[1]) <= 1 and
                    abs(fixed(g[2], 32) - w[2]) <= 64)
        checked += 1
        matched += close == n and n > 0
        if checked <= 8:
            print('run %d: %d decoded, %d sent, %d close; first decoded %s sent %s' %
                  (checked, len(got), len(want), close, [round(c, 1) for c in got[0]] if got else None,
                   (want[0][0] / 16.0, want[0][1] / 16.0, want[0][2]) if want else None))
        if checked >= limit:
            break
    print('%d runs checked, %d fully matched, %d skipped (set-up routine not done yet)' % (checked, matched, skipped))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

```

`tools/vp2_meshes.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""VP2's meshes converted offline to a native format, for the native renderer to load by where the game loaded them.

VP2 keeps its geometry only as prebuilt VIF packets inside its model files ('FPS\\0' chunks: first mesh at model + 0x60,
each mesh one packet whose size is the s16 at +4; docs/vp2_models.md): the unpack codes put a header, the strip
lengths and the vertices (4 qwords each: normal, UV, colour, position) into VU memory, and a material block (rows
7..21) when the mesh carries one. Those packets are read here, once, from the user's own disc (tools/extract.py
--assets); while the game runs nothing reads them: the renderer takes the files this writes (rule decision
2026-10-31, as for the textures). The set-up routines that only change the format (6, 8: fixed point to float) are
applied here; the others (skinning 2 / 4, fade 44, UV scroll 48) depend on the frame and are kept with their data;
12, 14, 18, 28, 32 and 46 do nothing (their table entries only return).

  tools/vp2_meshes.py [--assets build/extract/assets] [--out build/vp2_meshes]

Output (game data: build tree only, never committed): <out>/<asset part>.msh (the asset file's name without its
extension), a list of meshes:
  u32 magic 'VPM2', u32 mesh count, then per mesh:
    u32 offset of the mesh in the asset file, u32 vertex count, u32 strip count, u32 flags (header 2.x),
    i32 group mask (header 6.y), i32 routines[4], i32 routine data offsets[4] (rows from the mesh's top),
    f32 bounding centre[3], u32 has material, u32 header rows 8..12[20] (8.y the lighting mode),
    material (16 u32 words when it has one: ALPHA lo/hi, TEX0 lo/hi, strip tag PRIM, cull flag, texture index, then
      the raw rows' x lanes 7..15 for what is not decoded yet),
    u16 strip lengths[strip count] (padded to 4 bytes),
    vertices: f32 position[4] (w: the bone's VU address for routine 2), f32 normal[3], f32 uv[2], u8 rgba[4],
    for routine 4: per vertex i32 bone a, f32 weight a, i32 bone b, f32 weight b.
    for routine 48 (texture scrolling): i32 the offset's row index (the UV offset is .zw of object row 38 + index).
"""
import argparse
import os
import struct
import sys
from collections import Counter


class Vu:
    """The 1024 quadwords one mesh's unpack codes write (lanes as raw 32-bit words), TOPS = 0."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.mem = [[0, 0, 0, 0] for _ in range(1024)]
        self.cl, self.wl = 1, 1
        self.mask = 0
        self.row = [0, 0, 0, 0]

    def i(self, qw, lane):
        v = self.mem[qw % 1024][lane] & 0xFFFFFFFF
        return v - (1 << 32) if v & 0x80000000 else v

    def f(self, qw, lane):
        return struct.unpack('<f', struct.pack('<I', self.mem[qw % 1024][lane] & 0xFFFFFFFF))[0]

    def setf(self, qw, lane, value):
        self.mem[qw % 1024][lane] = struct.unpack('<I', struct.pack('<f', value))[0]


def unpack(vu, data, at, end):
    """Runs a mesh packet's VIF codes into vu. False on a code this tool does not know."""
    p = at
    while p + 4 <= end:
        w, = struct.unpack_from('<I', data, p)
        cmd, num, imm = (w >> 24) & 0x7F, (w >> 16) & 0xFF, w & 0xFFFF
        p += 4
        if (cmd & 0x60) == 0x60:
            vn, vl = (cmd >> 2) & 3, cmd & 3
            n = num or 256
            unsigned = (imm >> 14) & 1
            masked = (cmd >> 4) & 1
            addr = imm & 0x3FF
            comps = vn + 1
            if vl == 3:  # V4-5
                nbytes = 2 * n
            else:
                nbytes = comps * (32 >> vl) // 8 * n
            for k in range(n):
                qw = (addr + (k // vu.wl) * vu.cl + k % vu.wl) % 1024
                values = []
                if vl == 3:
                    v, = struct.unpack_from('<H', data, p + 2 * k)
                    values = [(v & 0x1F) << 3, ((v >> 5) & 0x1F) << 3, ((v >> 10) & 0x1F) << 3, ((v >> 15) & 1) << 7]
                else:
                    size = 32 >> vl
                    fmt = {32: 'I', 16: 'H', 8: 'B'}[size]
                    for c in range(comps):
                        raw, = struct.unpack_from('<' + fmt, data, p + (k * comps + c) * size // 8)
                        if size < 32 and not unsigned and raw >> (size - 1):
                            raw -= 1 << size
                        values.append(raw & 0xFFFFFFFF)
                    if comps == 1:  # S: one value to every lane
                        values = values * 4
                    elif comps < 4:
                        values += [vu.mem[qw][c] for c in range(comps, 4)]
                for c in range(4):
                    if masked:
                        m = (vu.mask >> (2 * c + 8 * min(k % vu.wl, 3))) & 3
                        if m == 1:
                            vu.mem[qw][c] = vu.row[c]
                            continue
                        if m in (2, 3):
                            continue
                    vu.mem[qw][c] = values[c]
            p += (nbytes + 3) // 4 * 4
            continue
        if cmd == 0x01:  # STCYCL
            vu.cl, vu.wl = imm & 0xFF, (imm >> 8) & 0xFF
            if vu.wl == 0:
                vu.wl = 1
        elif cmd == 0x20:  # STMASK
            vu.mask, = struct.unpack_from('<I', data, p)
            p += 4
        elif cmd == 0x30:  # STROW
            vu.row = list(struct.unpack_from('<4I', data, p))
            p += 16
        elif cmd == 0x31:  # STCOL
            p += 16
        elif cmd in (0x00, 0x02, 0x03, 0x04, 0x05, 0x06, 0x07, 0x10, 0x11, 0x13):
            pass
        else:
            return False
    return True


def material(vu):
    """The material block (rows 7..21) the mesh carries: blend, texture template, strip PRIM, cull, texture index."""
    alpha = (vu.mem[8][0], vu.mem[8][1])
    tex0 = (vu.mem[9][0], vu.mem[9][1])
    prim = (vu.mem[13][1] >> 15) & 0x7FF  # the strip tag's PRIM (PRE set)
    cull = vu.mem[15][0]
    texture = vu.mem[15][2]
    rest = [vu.mem[r][0] for r in range(7, 16)]
    return [alpha[0], alpha[1], tex0[0], tex0[1], prim, cull, texture] + rest


def convert(data, at, size):
    vu = Vu()
    if not unpack(vu, data, at, at + size):
        return None, 'unknown VIF code'
    top = 0
    vertices = top + vu.i(top, 1)
    strips = vu.i(top, 2)
    lengths = top + vu.i(top, 3)
    count = vu.i(top + 2, 1)
    flags = vu.i(top + 2, 0)
    group = vu.i(top + 6, 1)
    if not (0 < count < 1024 and 0 < strips < 1024):
        return None, 'bad header'
    routines = [vu.i(top + 5, r) for r in range(4)]
    bases = [vu.i(top + 4, r) for r in range(4)]
    for r in routines:  # the format conversions
        if r == 8:
            for k in range(count):
                v = vertices + 4 * k
                for c in range(3):
                    vu.setf(v, c, vu.i(v, c) / 4096.0)
                    vu.setf(v + 3, c, vu.i(v + 3, c) / 16.0)
        elif r == 6:
            for k in range(count):
                v = vertices + 4 * k
                for c in range(3):
                    vu.setf(v, c, vu.i(v, c) / 4096.0)
        # 12..54 except 20, 26, 30, 36, 40, 44, 48: their set-up table entries only return (JR) in every build of the microcode.
        elif r not in (0, 12, 14, 16, 18, 22, 24, 28, 32, 34, 38, 42, 46, 50, 52, 54, 2, 4, 36, 44, 48):
            return None, f'routine {r}'
    out = bytearray()
    has_material = 1 if flags & 1 else 0
```
